Summary\n+\n+## Overview\n+\n+RiotPrompt has evolved into a comprehensive prompt engineering library with multiple distinct concerns. This plan splits the monolithic codebase into **flat peer projects** within the kjerneverk workspace, with clear separation between product-specific and shared libraries.\n+\n+## Architecture Philosophy\n+\n+### Flat Peer Projects (Not Monorepo)\n+Every project is a **sibling directory** in `/Users/tobrien/gitw/kjerneverk/`. No nested packages, no workspace linking—just independent npm packages with explicit peer dependencies.\n+\n+### Product-Specific vs Shared\n+- **Product-specific**: Prefixed with product name (`riotprompt-*`, `riotplan-*`)\n+- **Shared**: Generic names, usable across products (`agentic`, `execution-*`)\n+\n+## Target Directory Structure\n+\n+```\n+/Users/tobrien/gitw/kjerneverk/\n+│\n+├── riotprompt/              # Core prompt modeling (THE original mission)\n+│                            # Role vs Command vs Context clarity\n+│                            # Templates, formatting, serialization, recipes\n+│\n+├── riotprompt-cli/          # CLI for prompt operations\n+│\n+├── riotplan/                # Plans framework (FUTURE - stubbed)\n+│                            # Long-lived stateful workflows\n+│                            # STATUS.md, phases, interruption recovery\n+│\n+├── riotplan-cli/            # CLI for plan operations (FUTURE - stubbed)\n+│\n+├── agentic/                 # SHARED: Agentic framework\n+│                            # ConversationBuilder, ToolRegistry\n+│                            # IterationStrategy, Reflection\n+│\n+├── execution/               # SHARED: Execution interfaces\n+│                            # Provider contracts, ModelRegistry\n+│                            # No SDK dependencies\n+│\n+├── execution-openai/        # SHARED: OpenAI provider\n+│                            # Only depends on: openai, tiktoken\n+│\n+├── execution-anthropic/     # SHARED: Anthropic provider\n+│                            # Only depends on: @anthropic-ai/sdk\n+│\n+├── execution-gemini/        # SHARED: Gemini provider\n+│                            # Only depends on: @google/generative-ai\n+│\n+└── prompts/                 # Prompt/plan files (not a package)\n+```\n+\n+## Dependency Graph\n+\n+```\n+                    ┌──────────────┐     ┌──────────────┐\n+                    │ riotprompt   │     │  riotplan    │\n+                    │    -cli      │     │    -cli      │\n+                    └──────┬───────┘     └──────┬───────┘\n+                           │                    │\n+         ┌─────────────────┼────────────────────┤\n+         │                 │                    │\n+         ▼                 ▼                    ▼\n+┌──────────────┐   ┌──────────────┐    ┌──────────────┐\n+│ riotprompt   │   │   agentic    │    │  riotplan    │\n+│              │   │   (shared)   │    │              │\n+└──────────────┘   └──────┬───────┘    └──────┬───────┘\n+                          │                   │\n+                          ▼                   │\n+                   ┌──────────────┐           │\n+                   │  execution   │ ◄─────────┘\n+                   │ (interfaces) │\n+                   └──────┬───────┘\n+                          │\n+         ┌────────────────┼────────────────┐\n+         │                │                │\n+         ▼                ▼                ▼\n+   ┌──────────┐    ┌──────────┐    ┌──────────┐\n+   │ exec-    │    │  exec-   │    │  exec-   │\n+   │ openai   │    │anthropic │    │  gemini  │\n+   └──────────┘    └──────────┘    └──────────┘\n+```\n+\n+## Package Responsibilities\n+\n+### riotprompt (Core Prompt Modeling)\n+The **original mission**: making prompt structure clear and manageable.\n+\n+**Contains:**\n+- Items: Content, Context, Instruction, Section, Trait, Weighted, Parameters\n+- Prompt definition and creation\n+- Formatter (model-agnostic output)\n+- Parser (markdown, text input)\n+- Loader (file/directory loading)\n+- Serializer (JSON/XML)\n+- Recipes (cook, templates)\n+- Override system\n+\n+**Standalone Value:** Structure your prompts without any execution or agentic concerns.\n+\n+### agentic (Shared Agentic Framework)\n+Multi-turn conversations and tool orchestration.\n+\n+**Contains:**\n+- ConversationBuilder\n+- ContextManager\n+- TokenBudgetManager, TokenCounter\n+- MessageBuilder\n+- ToolRegistry\n+- IterationStrategy, StrategyExecutor\n+- Reflection, MetricsCollector\n+\n+**Standalone Value:** Build AI agents with tool use, works with any prompt library.\n+\n+### execution (Shared Interfaces)\n+Provider contracts and model configuration.\n+\n+**Contains:**\n+- Provider interface\n+- ExecutionOptions, ProviderResponse types\n+- ModelRegistry\n+- Model family detection\n+- Encoding/tokenizer mapping\n+\n+**Standalone Value:** Define execution contracts without SDK dependencies.\n+\n+### execution-openai / execution-anthropic / execution-gemini\n+Individual provider implementations.\n+\n+**Contains (each):**\n+- Provider implementation\n+- SDK-specific transformations\n+- Model-specific handling\n+\n+**Standalone Value:** Install only the providers you use.\n+\n+### riotplan (Future - Stubbed)\n+Long-lived stateful AI workflows.\n+\n+A **plan** bridges the gap between prompts (single interaction) and agentic conversations (multi-turn sessions). Plans manage work that:\n+- Spans multiple sessions/days/weeks\n+- Has persistent state tracked in files\n+- Is organized into numbered steps (01-STEP.md, 02-STEP.md)\n+- Can be interrupted and resumed\n+- Supports human review and feedback loops\n+\n+**Plan Structure:**\n+```\n+my-plan/\n+├── my-plan-prompt.md     # Meta-prompt (prompt-of-prompts)\n+├── SUMMARY.md            # Overview of approach\n+├── EXECUTION_PLAN.md     # Step-by-step strategy\n+├── STATUS.md             # Current state (auto-updated)\n+├── plan/                 # Step files\n+│   ├── 01-first-step.md\n+│   ├── 02-second-step.md\n+│   └── ...\n+└── analysis/             # Analysis output (optional)\n+```\n+\n+**Real Examples:**\n+- `/Users/tobrien/gitw/kjerneverk/prompts/big-splitup/` - This plan!\n+- `/Users/tobrien/gitw/grunnverk/prompts/parallel-execution/`\n+- `/Users/tobrien/gitw/grunnverk/prompts/commit-splitting/`\n+\n+**Will Contain:**\n+- Plan loading and creation\n+- Step discovery and sequencing\n+- STATUS.md parsing and generation\n+- State management and updates\n+- Interruption recovery\n+- Type definitions for plans, steps, phases\n+\n+**Standalone Value:** Orchestrate multi-step AI workflows with persistence.\n+\n+## Benefits of This Architecture\n+\n+1. **No Dependency Bloat**: Install `execution-openai` without pulling in Anthropic/Gemini SDKs\n+2. **Clear Boundaries**: Each project has one job\n+3. **Independent Versioning**: `agentic` can evolve without touching `riotprompt`\n+4. **Reusability**: `agentic` and `execution-*` work with any prompt library\n+5. **Future-Proof**: `riotplan` can build on these foundations\n+6. **Simple Structure**: No monorepo complexity, just npm packages\n+\n+## Migration Path\n+\n+1. **Phase 1**: Extract shared libraries (`execution`, `execution-*`, `agentic`)\n+2. **Phase 2**: Slim down `riotprompt` to core prompt concerns\n+3. **Phase 3**: Create `riotprompt-cli` as separate project\n+4. **Phase 4**: Stub out `riotplan` and `riotplan-cli`\n+5. **Phase 5**: Update all docs and release\n+\n+## Success Criteria\n+\n+1. Each project builds and tests independently\n+2. `npm install riotprompt` still works (re-exports from dependencies)\n+3. `npm install execution-openai` works without other SDKs\n+4. Existing code using `riotprompt` continues to work\n+5. Clear documentation for new structure\n+\n+## Timeline Estimate\n+\n+- **Phase 1** (Steps 01-04): Shared library extraction - 3-4 days\n+- **Phase 2** (Steps 05-07): RiotPrompt slimming - 2-3 days\n+- **Phase 3** (Steps 08-09): CLI and riotplan stubs - 1-2 days\n+- **Phase 4** (Steps 10-11): Integration and release - 1-2 days\n+\n+**Total: ~8-11 working days**\ndiff --git a/prompts/big-splitup/big-splitup-prompt.md b/prompts/big-splitup/big-splitup-prompt.md\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..f536e53\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/prompts/big-splitup/big-splitup-prompt.md\n@@ -0,0 +1,15 @@\n+Ok so this is going to be a big tasks, but I think that RiotPrompt has reached a level of complexity that probably deserves a big of a split up strategy. Create the big-splitup plan\n+2. Revise plan based on my feedback\n+3. `agentic/src/util/storage.ts` |\n+\n+### 3.4 Update Imports\n+\n+All files need to import from `execution` instead of local:\n+\n+```typescript\n+// Before\n+import { Model } from \"./chat\";\n+import { getPersonaRole } from \"./model-config\";\n+\n+// After\n+import type { Model } from \"execution\";\n+import { getPersonaRole } from \"execution\";\n+```\n+\n+### 3.5 Handle Formatter independent of riotprompt while allowing integration.\n+\n+### 3.6 Create Package Index\n+\n+Create `agentic/src/index.ts`:\n+\n+```typescript\n+// ===== CONVERSATION MANAGEMENT =====\n+export { ConversationBuilder } from \"./conversation\";\n+export { ContextManager } from \"./context-manager\";\n+export { MessageBuilder, MessageTemplates } from \"./message-builder\";\n+export { ConversationLogger, ConversationReplayer } from \"./conversation-logger\";\n+\n+// ===== TOKEN MANAGEMENT =====\n+export { TokenCounter, TokenBudgetManager } from \"./token-budget\";\n+\n+// ===== TOOL INTEGRATION =====\n+export { ToolRegistry } from \"./tools\";\n+\n+// ===== ITERATION STRATEGIES =====\n+export { StrategyExecutor, IterationStrategyFactory } from \"./iteration-strategy\";\n+\n+// ===== OBSERVABILITY =====\n+export { MetricsCollector, ReflectionReportGenerator } from \"./reflection\";\n+\n+// ===== TYPES =====\n+export type {\n+    ConversationMessage,\n+    ConversationBuilderConfig,\n+    ConversationMetadata,\n+    ConversationState,\n+    InjectOptions,\n+    ToolCall\n+} from \"./conversation\";\n+\n+export type {\n+ CompressionStrategy\n+} from \"./token-budget\";\n+\n+export type {\n+    SemanticRole,\n+    MessageMetadata\n+} from \"./message-builder\";\n+\n+export type {\n+    IterationStrategy,\n+    StrategyPhase,\n+    StrategyState,\n+    StrategyResult,\n+    StrategyContext,\n+    PhaseResult,\n+    ToolResult,\n+    LLMClient,\n+    ToolUsagePolicy,\n+    Insight\n+} from \"./iteration-strategy\";\n+\n+export type {\n+    ReflectionReport,\n+    ReflectionConfig,\n+    AgenticExecutionMetrics,\n+    ToolExecutionMetric,\n+    ToolStats,\n+    Recommendation,\n+    ToolEffectivenessAnalysis,\n+    PerformanceInsights,\n+    QualityAssessment\n+} from \"./reflection\";\n+\n+export type {\n+    LogConfig,\n+    LogFormat,\n+    LoggedConversation,\n+    ConversationLogMetadata,\n+    LoggedMessage,\n+    ToolCallLog,\n+    ConversationSummary,\n+    ReplayOptions,\n+    ReplayResult\n+} from \"./conversation-logger\";\n+\n+export type {\n+    Tool,\n+    ToolParameter,\n+    ToolContext,\n+    ToolExample,\n+    ToolCost,\n+    OpenAITool,\n+    AnthropicTool,\n+    ToolDefinition,\n+    ToolUsageStats\n+} from \"./tools\";\n+```\n+\n+### 3.7 Create README\n+\n+Create `agentic/README.md`:\n+\n+```markdown\n+# agentic\n+\n+Agentic AI framework for multi-turn conversations, tool integration, and execution strategies.\n+\n+## Features\n+\n+- **ConversationBuilder**: Manage multi-turn conversations with context tracking\n+- **ToolRegistry**: Define, register, and execute tools\n+- **IterationStrategies**: Pre-built strategies for agentic workflows\n+- **Reflection**: Metrics collection and execution analysis\n+\n+## Installation\n+\n+```bash\n+npm install agentic execution\n+```\n+\n+## Quick Start\n+\n+```typescript\n+import { ConversationBuilder, ToolRegistry, IterationStrategyFactory } from 'agentic';\n+\n+// Create conversation\n+const conversation = ConversationBuilder.create({ model: 'gpt-4o' })\n+    .addSystemMessage('You are a research assistant')\n+    .addUserMessage('Find information about X')\n+    .build();\n+\n+// Register tools\n+const tools = ToolRegistry.create();\n+tools.register({\n+    name: 'search',\n+    description: 'Search for information',\n+    parameters: {\n+        type: 'object',\n+        properties: {\n+            query: { type: 'string', description: 'Search query' }\n+        },\n+        required: ['query']\n+    },\n+    execute: async ({ query }) => searchAPI(query)\n+});\n+\n+// Create strategy\n+const strategy createRequest,\n+    getModelFamily \n+} from 'execution';\n+\n+// From execution providers (pick based on model)\n+import { OpenAIProvider } from 'execution-openai';\n+import { AnthropicProvider } from 'execution-anthropic';\n+import { GeminiProvider } from 'execution-gemini';\n+\n+// From agentic (optional, for future agent command)\n+import { \n+    ConversationBuilder, \n+    ToolRegistry,\n+    IterationStrategyFactory\n+} from 'agentic';\n+\n+config(); // Load .env\n+\n+// ... rest of CLI implementation\n+```\n+\n+### 6.5 Create ExecutionManager\n+\n+Create `riotprompt-cli/src/execution-manager.ts`:\n+\n+```typescript\n+import type { Provider, Request, ExecutionOptions, ProviderResponse } from 'execution';\n+import { getModelFamily } from 'execution';\n+\n+// Dynamic provider loading\n+let openaiProvider: Provider | null = null;\n+let anthropicProvider: Provider | null = null;\n+let geminiProvider: Provider | null = null;\n+\n+async function getProvider(model: string): Promise<Provider> {\n+    const family = getModelFamily(model);\n+    \n+    switch (family) {\n+        case 'openai':\n+            if (!openaiProvider) {\n+                const { OpenAIProvider } = await import('execution-openai');\n+                openaiProvider = new OpenAIProvider();\n+            }\n+            return openaiProvider;\n+            \n+        case 'anthropic':\n+            if (!anthropicProvider) {\n+                const { AnthropicProvider } = await import('execution-anthropic');\n+                anthropicProvider = new AnthropicProvider();\n+            }\n+            return anthropicProvider;\n+            \n+        case 'gemini':\n+            if (!geminiProvider) {\n+                const { GeminiProvider } = await import('execution-gemini');\n+                geminiProvider = new GeminiProvider();\n+            }\n+            return geminiProvider;\n+            \n+        default:\n+            throw new Error(`Unknown model family: ${family}`);\n+    }\n+}\n+\n+export async function execute(\n+    request: Request, \n+    options: ExecutionOptions = {}\n+): Promise<ProviderResponse> {\n+    const model = options.model || request.model;\n+    const provider = await getProvider(model);\n+    return provider.execute(request, options);\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 6.6 Update CLI Commands\n+\n+**create command:**\n+```typescript\n+program\n+    .command('create <name>')\n+    .description('Create a new prompt structure')\n+    .option('--persona <text>', 'Initial persona')\n+    .option('--import <file>', 'Import from existing file')\n+    .action(async (name, options) => {\n+        await Writer.scaffold(name, options);\n+        console.log(`✅ Created prompt structure: ${name}`);\n+    });\n+```\n+\n+**process command:**\n+```typescript\n+program\n+    .command('process <path>')\n+    .description('Process a prompt directory')\n+    .option('-m, --model <model>', 'Target model', 'gpt-4')\n+    .option('-f, --format <format>', 'Output format (text|json|xml)', 'text')\n+    .option('-o, --output <file>', 'Output file')\n+    .action(async (path, options) => {\n+        const prompt = await Loader.loadPrompt(path);\n+        const formatter = Formatter.create();\n+        \n+        let output: string;\n+        if (options.format === 'json') {\n+            output = Serializer.toJSON(prompt);\n+        } else if (options.format === 'xml') {\n+            output = Serializer.toXML(prompt);\n+        } else {\n+            const request = formatter.formatPrompt(options.model, prompt);\n+            output = JSON.stringify(request, null, 2);\n+        }\n+        \n+        if (options.output) {\n+            await Writer.write(options.output, output);\n+            console.log(`✅ Wrote to ${options.output}`);\n+        } else {\n+            console.log(output);\n+        }\n+    });\n+```\n+\n+**execute command:**\n+```typescript\n+program\n+    .command('execute <path>')\n+    .description('Execute a prompt against an LLM')\n+    .option('-m, --model <model>', 'Model to use', 'gpt-4')\n+    .option('-k, --api-key <key>', 'API key (overrides env)')\n+    .option('-t, --temperature <temp>', 'Temperature', '0.7')\n+    .option('--max-tokens <n>', 'Max tokens')\n+    .option('--dry-run', 'Show request without executing')\n+    .action(async (path, options) => {\n+        const prompt = await Loader.loadPrompt(path);\n+        const formatter = Formatter.create();\n+        const request = formatter.formatPrompt(options.model, prompt);\n+        \n+        if (options.dryRun) {\n+            console.log('Request:', JSON.stringify(request, null, 2));\n+            return;\n+        }\n+        \n+        const response = await execute(request, {\n+            model: options.model,\n+            apiKey: options.apiKey,\n+            temperature: parseFloat(options.temperature),\n+            maxTokens: options.maxTokens ? parseInt(options.maxTokens) : undefined,\n+        });\n+        \n+        console.log(response.content);\n+    });\n+```\n+\n+### 6.7 Create Vite Config\n+\n+Create `riotprompt-cli/vite.config.ts`:\n+\n+```typescript\n+import { defineConfig } from 'vite';\n+import { preserveShebang } from 'rollup-plugin-preserve-shebang';\n+\n+export default defineConfig({\n+    build: {\n+        lib: {\n+            entry: 'src/cli.ts',\n+            formats: ['es'],\n+            fileName: () => file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..56f9e07\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/prompts/big-splitup/plan/08-riotplan-stub.md\n@@ -0,0 +1,385 @@\n+# Step 08: RiotPlan Stub\n+\n+## Objective\n+\n+Create `riotplan` as a properly structured project for managing long-lived, stateful AI workflows. This step establishes the types, conventions, and stub implementations based on real-world plan examples.\n+\n+## Project Location\n+\n+```\n+/Users/tobrien/gitw/kjerneverk/riotplan/\n+```\n+\n+## What is RiotPlan?\n+\n+RiotPlan is a framework for **long-lived, stateful AI workflows** (plans). Unlike prompts (single interaction) or agentic conversations (multi-turn session), plans:\n+\n+- Span multiple sessions/days/weeks\n+- Have a directory structure that IS the plan\n+- Use numbered files for sequential steps (01-STEP.md, 02-STEP.md)\n+- Track state persistently in STATUS.md\n+- Can be interrupted and resumed\n+- Support human review and feedback loops\n+\n+## Plan Structure (from Real Examples)\n+\n+Based on analysis of real plans:\n+\n+```\n+my-plan/\n+├── my-plan-prompt.md     # Meta-prompt (prompt-of-prompts)\n+├── SUMMARY.md            # Overview of the approach\n+├── EXECUTION_PLAN.md     # Step-by-step execution strategy\n+├── STATUS.md             # Current state (auto-updated)\n+├── README.md             # Navigation guide (optional)\n+├── plan/                 # Step files (optional subdirectory)\n+│   ├── 01-first-step.md\n+│   ├── 02-second-step.md\n+│   └── ...\n+├── analysis/             # Analysis output (optional)\n+├── architecture/         # Design docs (optional)\n+├── implementation/       # Implementation details (optional)\n+└── testing/              # Test strategies (optional)\n+```\n+\n+### Real Examples\n+\n+- `/Users/tobrien/gitw/kjerneverk/prompts/big-splitup/` - This plan!\n+- `/Users/tobrien/gitw/grunnverk/prompts/big-splitup/` - Larger example with phases\n+- `/Users/tobrien/gitw/grunnverk/prompts/parallel-execution/` - Complex feature\n+- `/Users/tobrien/gitw/grunnverk/prompts/commit-splitting/` - Feature plan\n+- `/Users/tobrien/gitw/grunnverk/prompts/shared-utils/` - Package extraction\n+- `/Users/tobrien/gitw/grunnverk/prompts/ai-service/` - Service extraction\n+\n+## Tasks\n+\n+### 8.1 Create Project Structure\n+\n+Directory already exists at `/Users/tobrien/gitw/kjerneverk/riotplan/`\n+\n+```bash\n+mkdir -p /Users/tobrien/gitw/kjerneverk/riotplan/src\n+mkdir -p /Users/tobrien/gitw/kjerneverk/riotplan/tests\n+```\n+\n+### 8.2 Create Type Definitions\n+\n+Create `riotplan/src/types.ts` with types based on real plan patterns:\n+\n+```typescript\n+/**\n+ * RiotPlan Type Definitions\n+ *\n+ * Types for long-lived, stateful AI workflows (plans).\n+ *\n+ * Key concepts:\n+ * - A plan has a directory (the plan code/name)\n+ * - A meta-prompt initiates the plan\n+ * - Numbered step files (01-STEP.md) define the sequence\n+ * - STATUS.md tracks progress\n+ * - EXECUTION_PLAN.md documents strategy\n+ */\n+\n+// Task status (matches checkbox states)\n+export type TaskStatus =\n+  | \"pending\"      // ⬜ Not started\n+  | \"in_progress\"  // 🔄 Currently active\n+  | \"completed\"    // ✅ Done\n+  | \"failed\"       // ❌ Failed with error\n+  | \"blocked\"      // ⏸️ Waiting on dependency\n+  | \"skipped\";     // ⏭️ Intentionally skipped\n+\n+// A single step (corresponds to 01-STEP.md file)\n+export interface PlanStep {\n+  number: number;           // Step number (1, 2, 3...)\n+  code: string;             // Step code (e.g., \"execution-interfaces\")\n+  filename: string;         // Full filename\n+  title: string;            // Human-readable title\n+  description?: string;\n+  status: TaskStatus;\n+  dependencies?: number[];  // Step numbers this depends on\n+  startedAt?: Date;\n+  completedAt?: Date;\n+  duration?: number;\n+  notes?: string;\n+  filePath: string;\n+}\n+\n+// Plan metadata\n+export interface PlanMetadata {\n+  code: string;             // Directory name (e.g., \"big-splitup\")\n+  name: string;             // Human-readable name\n+  description?: string;\n+  version?: string;\n+  author?: string;\n+  tags?: string[];\n+  createdAt?: Date;\n+  path: string;             // Directory path\n+}\n+\n+// File discovery\n+export interface PlanFiles {\n+  metaPrompt?: string;      // {code}-prompt.md or prompt-of-prompts.md\n+  summary?: string;         // SUMMARY.md\n+  status?: string;          // STATUS.md\n+  executionPlan?: string;   // EXECUTION_PLAN.md\n+  readme?: string;          // README.md\n+  steps: string[];          // Numbered step files in order\n+  analysisDir?: string;     // analysis/ directory\n+  subdirectories: string[]; // Other directories\n+}\n+\n+// Plan state (from STATUS.md)\n+export interface PlanState {\n+  status: TaskStatus;\n+  currentStep?: number;\n+  lastCompletedStep?: number;\n+  startedAt?: Date;\n+  lastUpdatedAt: Date;\n+  completedAt?: Date;\n+  blockers: Blocker[];\n+  issues: Issue[];\n+  progress: number;         // 0-100 percentage\n+}\n+\n+// Complete plan\n+export interface Plan {\n+  metadata: PlanMetadata;\n+  files: PlanFiles;\n+  steps: PlanStep[];\n+  phases?: PlanPhase[];     // Optional grouping\n+  state: PlanState;\n+}\n+\n+// ... additional types for Blocker, Issue, PlanPhase, etc.\n+```\n+\n+### 8.3 Create Index with Stub Functions\n+\n+Create `riotplan/src/index.ts`:\n+\n+```typescript\n+/**\n+ * RiotPlan - Framework for long-lived, stateful AI workflows\n+ *\n+ * @example Plan directory structure:\n+ * ```\n+ * my-plan/\n+ * ├── my-plan-prompt.md     # Meta-prompt\n+ * ├── SUMMARY.md            # Overview\n+ * ├── EXECUTION_PLAN.md     # Strategy\n+ * ├── STATUS.md             # Current state\n+ * └── plan/\n+ *     ├── 01-first-step.md\n+ *     ├── 02-second-step.md\n+ *     └── ...\n+ * ```\n+ */\n+\n+// Export types\n+export type { TaskStatus, PlanStep, Plan, PlanMetadata, PlanFiles, PlanState } from `/Users/tobrien/gitw/kjerneverk/riotprompt/README.md`:\n+\n+```markdown\n+# RiotPrompt\n+\n+Get it together, and organize your prompts.\n+\n+## What is RiotPrompt?\n+\n+RiotPrompt brings structure and clarity to prompt engineering. Instead of sprawling text files, you get:\n+\n+- **Clear separation** of persona, instructions, context, and content\n+- **Type-safe** prompt construction\n+- **Model-agnostic** formatting\n+- **Template system** for reusable patterns\n+\n+## Installation\n+\n+```bash\n+npm install riotprompt\n+```\n+\n+For CLI tools:\n+```bash\n+npm install -g riotprompt-cli\n+```\n+\n+## Quick Start\n+\n+```typescript\n+import { cook, Formatter } from 'riotprompt';\n+\n+const prompt = await cook({\n+  basePath: __dirname,\n+  persona: { content: 'You are a helpful code reviewer' },\n+  instructions: [\n+    { content: 'Review code for bugs and improvements' },\n+    { content: 'Be constructive and specific' },\n+  ],\n+  context: [\n+    { title: 'Project Context', content: 'This is a TypeScript project' },\n+  ],\n+});\n+\n+const formatter = Formatter.create();\n+const request = formatter.formatPrompt('gpt-4o', prompt);\n+```\n+\n+## Ecosystem\n+\n+RiotPrompt is part of a larger ecosystem:\n+\n+| Package | Description |\n+|---------|-------------|\n+| **riotprompt** | Core prompt modeling |\n+| **riotprompt-cli** | CLI for prompt operations |\n+| **agentic** | Multi-turn conversations & tools |\n+| **execution** | LLM provider interfaces |\n+| **execution-openai** | OpenAI provider |\n+| **execution-anthropic** | Anthropic provider |\n+| **execution-gemini** | Gemini provider |\n+| **riotplan** | Long-lived workflows (coming) |\n+\n+## Importing Features\n+\n+```typescript\n+// Core prompt features (always available)\n+import { cook, Formatter, Loader, Serializer } from 'riotprompt';\n+\n+// Agentic features (re-exported)\n+import { ConversationBuilder, ToolRegistry } from 'riotprompt';\n+\n+// Execution features (re-exported)\n+import { getModelFamily, createRequest } from 'riotprompt';\n+\n+// Or import directly for smaller bundles\n+import { cook } from 'riotprompt';\n+import { ConversationBuilder } from 'agentic';\n+import { OpenAIProvider } from 'execution-openai';\n+```\n+\n+## Documentation\n+\n+- [API Reference](./docs/api-reference.md)\n+- [Recipes System](./docs/recipes.md)\n+- [Template Configuration](./docs/template-configuration.md)\n+\n+## Philosophy\n+\n+RiotPrompt is designed to be completely generic and unopinionated. Unlike other prompt libraries that assume specific use cases, RiotPrompt provides the building blocks for any prompt-based application while maintaining type safety.\n+\n+## License\n+\n+Apache-2.0\n+```\n+\n+### 10.2 Create Ecosystem Overview\n+\n+Create `/Users/tobrien/gitw/kjerneverk/ECOSYSTEM.md`:\n+\n+```markdown\n+# Kjerneverk Ecosystem\n+\n+This workspace contains Notes\n+\n+```markdown\n+# Release Notes: Kjerneverk Ecosystem v1.0\n+\n+## Overview\n+\n+We've restructured the riotprompt codebase into a modular ecosystem of packages.\n+\n+## New Packages\n+\n+- **execution** - Provider interfaces and model configuration\n+- **execution-openai** - OpenAI provider (GPT-4, etc.)\n+- **execution-anthropic** - Anthropic provider (Claude)\n+- **execution-gemini** - Gemini provider\n+- **agentic** - Agentic AI framework\n+- **riotprompt-cli** - Command-line interface\n+- **riotplan** (alpha) - Long-lived workflows\n+\n+## Benefits\n+\n+1. **Smaller bundles** - Install only what you need\n+2. **Clear boundaries** - Each package has one job\n+3. **Independent versioning** - Packages evolve independently\n+4. **Better reusability** - agentic/execution work with any prompt library\n+\n+## Migration\n+\n+No breaking changes! All existing riotprompt imports continue to work.\n+\n+See MIGRATION.md for optimization tips.\n+\n+## What's Next\n+\n+- riotplan development\n+- Additional execution providers\n+- Performance improvements\n+```\n+\n+### 11.8 Update STATUS.md\n+\n+Mark all steps as complete in `/Users/tobrien/gitw/kjerneverk/prompts/big-splitup/STATUS.md`.\n+\n+## Post-Release\n+\n+### Announce\n+\n+- Update documentation site\n+- Social media announcement\n+- GitHub release\n+\n+### Monitor\n+\n+- Watch for issues\n+- Respond to questions\n+- Collect feedback\n+\n+### Plan Next\n+\n+- riotplan development\n+- Feature requests\n+- Bug fixes\n+\n+## Completion\n+\n+🎉 **Congratulations!** The RiotPrompt split-up is complete!\n+\n+All packages are now:\n+- Independent projects in `/Users/tobrien/gitw/kjerneverk/`\n+- Published to npm\n+- Documented\n+- Ready for use\n+\n+## Final Directory Structure\n+\n+```\n+/Users/tobrien/gitw/kjerneverk/\n+├── riotprompt/           ✅ Published 1.0.0\n+├── riotprompt-cli/       ✅ Published 1.0.0\n+├── agentic/              ✅ Published 0.1.0\n+├── execution/            ✅ Published 0.1.0\n+├── execution-openai/     ✅ Published 0.1.0\n+├── execution-anthropic/  ✅ Published 0.1.0\n+├── execution-gemini/     ✅ Published 0.1.0\n+├── riotplan/             ✅ Published 0.0.1-alpha.0\n+├── riotplan-cli/         ✅ Published 0.0.1-alpha.0\n+├── prompts/              (Plan files)\n+└── ECOSYSTEM.md          (Overview doc)\n+```\ndiff --git a/prompts/implement-riotplan/EXECUTION_PLAN.md b/prompts/implement-riotplan/EXECUTION_PLAN.md\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..65f22c4\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/prompts/implement-riotplan/EXECUTION_PLAN.md\n@@ -0,0 +1,452 @@\n+# RiotPlan Implementation: Execution Plan\n+\n+> ## ⚠️ DEFINITION OF DONE\n+> \n+> **Nothing is complete until `npm run precommit` passes on ALL affected projects.**\n+> \n+> ```bash\n+> npm run precommit   # Runs: npm run lint && npm run test\n+> ```\n+> \n+> This is non-negotiable. Every step, every phase, every change.\n+\n+---\n+\n+## Strategy\n+\n+Implementation follows a **phased approach** where each phase delivers working functionality that can be used immediately. The core principle is **progressive enhancement**—RiotPlan adds capabilities without requiring changes to existing plans.\n+\n+**Key Architectural Decisions**:\n+\n+1. **Command Packages**: Separated from the CLI shell:\n+   - `riotplan-cli` is a thin shell that composes commands\n+   - `riotplan-commands-*` packages provide discrete command groups\n+   - Supporting libraries (`riotplan-history`, `riotplan-renderer`, `riotplan-templates`) provide non-CLI functionality\n+\n+2. **Shared Execution Packages**: RiotPlan reuses the existing `@riotprompt/execution-*` packages:\n+   - `@riotprompt/execution` - Core interfaces (Provider, Request, Response)\n+   - `@riotprompt/execution-anthropic` - Claude provider\n+   - `@riotprompt/execution-openai` - OpenAI provider\n+   - `@riotprompt/execution-gemini` - Gemini provider\n+   \n+   This creates a **shared integration point** between riotprompt and riotplan ecosystems.\n+\n+Phases are sequenced by dependency and value delivery:\n+\n+1. **Phase 1: Core Foundation** - Types, plan operations, feedback capture\n+2. **Phase 2: Status & Steps** - Status tracking, step manipulation\n+3. **Phase 3: CLI & Commands** - CLI shell, command packages (plan, status, step, feedback)\n+4. **Phase 4: Dependencies & Dashboard** - Cross-plan coordination\n+5. **Phase 5: Templates & Rendering** - Starter templates, document rendering\n+6. **Phase 6: Advanced Features** - History management, retrospectives\n+\n+## Prerequisites\n+\n+Before execution:\n+\n+- [ ] Ensure Node 24.x is installed\n+- [ ] Verify existing `@riotprompt/riotplan` and `@riotprompt/riotplan-cli` structure\n+- [ ] Review RIOTPLAN_ANALYSIS.md for detailed requirements\n+- [ ] Review PROPOSED_PROJECTS.md for project structure\n+- [ ] **Initialize all empty repositories** with infrastructure from riotprompt (see PROPOSED_PROJECTS.md)\n+\n+## Infrastructure Requirements\n+\n+**CRITICAL**: All new projects must be initialized with the same infrastructure as `riotprompt` before any implementation work begins:\n+\n+| Infrastructure | Source | Required |\n+|---------------|--------|----------|\n+| `.github/workflows/test.yml` | Copy from riotprompt | ✅ |\n+| `.github/workflows/npm-publish.yml` | Copy from riotprompt | ✅ |\n+| `.github/dependabot.yml` | Copy from riotprompt | ✅ |\n+| `.gitignore`, `.npmrc`, `.nvmrc` | Copy from riotprompt | ✅ |\n+| `tsconfig.json`, `eslint.config.mjs` | Copy from riotprompt | ✅ |\n+| `vite.config.ts`, `vitest.config.ts` | Adapt from riotprompt | ✅ |\n+| `LICENSE` (Apache 2.0) | Copy from riotprompt | ✅ |\n+| `src/`, `tests/`, `guide/` directories | Create new | ✅ |\n+| `package.json` with standard scripts | See PROPOSED_PROJECTS.md | ✅ |\n+| `guide/index.md` | Create new | ✅ |\n+| `README.md` | Create new | ✅ |\n+\n+**Repositories already created (need infrastructure)**:\n+- `commands-plan/`\n+- `commands-status/`\n+- `commands-feedback/`\n+- `commands-render/`\n+- `history/`\n+- `renderer/`\n+- `templates/`\n+\n+**Repositories still needed**:\n+- `commands-step/`\n+- `dashboard/`\n+\n+## Initial npm Publish Requirement\n+\n+**CRITICAL**: Each new package requires a **manual first publish** to npm before GitHub Actions can use trusted publishing.\n+\n+- **Token location**: `/Users/tobrien/gitw/kjerneverk/prompts/implement-riotplan/npm_token.txt`\n+- **Process**: See PROPOSED_PROJECTS.md \"Initial npm Publish\" section\n+- **After first publish**: Configure trusted publisher on npmjs.com for automated releases\n+\n+## Phase Breakdown\n+\n+### Phase 1: Core Foundation (Steps 01-04)\n+\n+**Goal**: Core plan infrastructure with feedback capture from day one\n+\n+**Projects Involved**:\n+- `riotplan` (enhance)\n+\n+| Step | Focus | Deliverable |\n+|------|-------|-------------|\n+| 01 | Type System Enhancement | Extended types for feedback, evidence, versioning |\n+| 02 | Plan Loading | `loadPlan()` - parse plan directories |\n+| 03 | Plan Creation | `createPlan()` - scaffold new plans |\n+| 04 | Feedback Capture | `createFeedback()` - preserve deliberation |\n+\n+**Quality Gate**: Can create, load, and validate a plan with feedback records\n+\n+### Phase 2: Status & Steps (Steps 05-08)\n+\n+**Goal**: Complete status tracking and step manipulation\n+\n+**Projects Involved**:\n+- `riotplan` (enhance)\n+\n+| Step | Focus | Deliverable |\n+|------|-------|-------------|\n+| 05 | STATUS.md Parsing | Parse existing STATUS.md files |\n+| 06 | STATUS.md Generation | Generate STATUS.md from plan state |\n+| 07 | Step Operations | Insert, remove, reorder steps with auto-renumbering |\n+| 08 | Plan Validation | Validate structure, numbering, dependencies |\n+\n+**Quality Gate**: Can manipulate plan structure and maintain valid STATUS.md\n+\n+### Phase 3: CLI & Command Packages (Steps 09-12)\n+\n+**Goal**: Usable CLI with modular command packages\n+\n+**Projects Involved**:\n+- `riotplan-cli` (refactor to shell)\n+- `riotplan-commands-plan` (create)\n+- `riotplan-commands-status` (create)\n+- `riotplan-commands-step` (create)\n+- `riotplan-commands-feedback` (create)\n+\n+| Step | Focus | Deliverable |\n+|------|-------|-------------|\n+| 09 | CLI Architecture | Refactor CLI to thin shell, shared utilities |\n+| 09a | Commands Package: Plan | `riotplan-commands-plan` with init, validate, archive |\n+| 10 | Commands Package: Status | `riotplan-commands-status` with show, list, dashboard, blockers |\n+| 11 | Commands Package: Step | `riotplan-commands-step` with add, remove, move, block, etc. |\n+| 12 | Commands Package: Feedback | `riotplan-commands-feedback` with create, list, show |\n+\n+**Quality Gate**: All Phase 1-2 features accessible via CLI with modular command structure\n+\n+### Phase 4: Dependencies & Web Dashboard (Steps 13-16)\n+\n+**Goal**: Cross-plan coordination and web-based dashboard\n+\n+**Projects Involved**:\n+- `riotplan` (enhance with dependency tracking)\n+- `riotplan-dashboard` (create - web server on port 8090)\n+\n+| Step | Focus | Deliverable |\n+|------|-------|-------------|\n+| 13 | Internal Dependencies | Dependency tracking within plans |\n+| 14 | External Dependencies | Cross-plan and cross-repo links |\n+| 15 | Plan Registry | Track plans across directories/repos |\n+| 16 | Web Dashboard | `riotplan-dashboard` - browser UI on port 8090 |\n+\n+**Quality Gate**: Can manage multiple related plans; web dashboard running\n+\n+### Phase 5: Templates & Rendering (Steps 17-20)\n+\n+**Goal**: Easy onboarding and shareable output\n+\n+**Projects Involved**:\n+- `riotplan-templates` (create)\n+- `riotplan-renderer` (create)\n+- `riotplan-commands-render` (create)\n+\n+| Step | Focus | Deliverable |\n+|------|-------|-------------|\n+| 17 | Starter Templates | `riotplan-templates` package with built-in templates |\n+| 18 | Template Commands | Add template commands to `riotplan-commands-plan` |\n+| 19 | Renderer Package | `riotplan-renderer` for PDF, HTML, DOCX generation |\n+| 20 | Render Commands | `riotplan-commands-render` package |\n+\n+**Quality Gate**: New users can start with templates; plans can be exported\n+\n+### Phase 6: Advanced Features (Steps 21-24)\n+\n+**Goal**: Complete feature set for power users\n+\n+**Projects Involved**:\n+- `riotplan-history` (create)\n+- `riotplan` (enhance with execution adapters)\n+- Uses existing: `execution`, `execution-anthropic`, `execution-openai`, `execution-gemini`\n+\n+| Step | Focus | Deliverable |\n+|------|-------|-------------|\n+| 21 | History Management | `riotplan-history` package with versioning, cleanup |\n+| 22 | Retrospectives | Completion reflection, RETROSPECTIVE.md |\n+| 23 | Execution Providers | Wrap `@riotprompt/execution-*` for step execution |\n+| 24 | Documentation Site | GitHub Pages documentation |\n+\n+**Quality Gate**: Full feature set documented and deployed\n+\n+**Note**: Step 23 reuses the existing `@riotprompt/execution-*` packages rather than creating new providers. This is the key integration point between riotprompt and riotplan.\n+\n+## Package Dependencies Summary\n+\n+```\n+Phase 3 creates these packages:\n+┌────────────────────┐\n+│   riotplan-cli     │ (core)\n+└────────────────────┘\n+\n+Phase 6 execution (reuses existing packages):\n+┌─────────────────┐\n+│    riotplan     │\n+│     (core)      │\n+└────────┬────────┘\n+         │ wraps via **CRITICAL**: Nothing is \"done\" until `npm run precommit` passes on ALL affected projects.\n+\n+```bash\n+# The precommit script runs:\n+npm run lint && npm run test\n+```\n+\n+This is the **single source of truth** for completion. Before marking any step, phase, or task as complete:\n+\n+1. Run `npm run precommit` in EVERY project that was modified\n+2. ALL projects must pass\n+3. No exceptions\n+\n+## Quality Gates (All Phases)\n+\n+### After EVERY Change\n+\n+```bash\n+# In each modified project:\n+npm run precommit   # MUST PASS - this is the definition of done\n+```\n+\n+### After Each Step\n+\n+1. **Precommit**: `npm run precommit` passes in ALL modified projects ✅ **REQUIRED**\n+2. **Build**: `npm run build` produces valid output\n+3. **Integration**: Works with existing plans in kjerneverk\n+\n+### After Each Phase\n+\n+1. **Precommit ALL**: Run `npm run precommit` in EVERY project in the workspace\n+2. **End-to-end test**: Complete workflow exercise\n+3. **Documentation**: README updated, guide files current\n+4. **Version bump**: Update package versions appropriately\n+\n+### Verification Script\n+\n+Run this to verify all projects pass:\n+\n+```bash\n+#!/bin/bash\n+# verify-all.sh - Run precommit on all projects\n+\n+PROJECTS=(\n+    \"riotplan\"\n+    \"riotplan-cli\"\n+    \"commands-plan\"\n+    \"commands-status\"\n+    \"commands-step\"\n+    \"commands-feedback\"\n+    \"commands-render\"\n+    \"history\"\n+    \"renderer\"\n+    \"templates\"\n+    \"dashboard\"\n+)\n+\n+FAILED=()\n+\n+for project in \"${PROJECTS[@]}\"; do\n+    dir=\"/Users/tobrien/gitw/kjerneverk/$project\"\n+    if [ -d \"$dir\" ] && [ -f \"$dir/package.json\" ]; then\n+        echo \"=== Checking $project ===\"\n+        cd \"$dir\"\n+        if ! npm run precommit; then\n+            FAILED+=(\"$project\")\n+        fi\n+    fi\n+done\n+\n+if [ ${#FAILED[@]} -eq 0 ]; then\n+    echo \"✅ ALL PROJECTS PASS\"\n+else\n+    echo \"❌ FAILED PROJECTS:\"\n+    printf '%s\\n' \"${FAILED[@]}\"\n+    exit 1\n+fi\n+```\n+\n+## Rollback Strategy\n+\n+Each step is atomic. If `npm run precommit` fails:\n+\n+1. **DO NOT** mark the step as complete\n+2. Revert to last passing state\n+3. Analyze failure cause\n+4. Fix the issue\n+5. Run `npm run precommit` again\n+6. Only proceed when it passes\n+\n+## Estimated Timeline\n+\n+| Phase | Steps | Estimated Duration |\n+|-------|-------|-------------------|\n+| Phase 1 | 01-04 | 2-3 days |\n+| Phase 2 | 05-08 | 2-3 days |\n+| Phase 3 | 09-12 | 3-4 days (more packages to create) |\n+| Phase 4 | 13-16 | 3-4 days |\n+| Phase 5 | 17-20 | 3-4 days |\n+| Phase 6 | 21-24 | 3-4 days |\n+\n+**Total**: ~16-22 days for v1.0\n+\n+## Dependencies Between Steps\n+\n+```\n+Phase 1: [01] → [02] → [03] → [04]\n+                 ↓\n+Phase 2: [05] → [06] → [07] → [08]\n+                 ↓\n+Phase 3: [09] → [09a] → [10] → [11] → [12]\n+                 ↓\n+Phase 4: [13] → [14] → [15] → [16]\n+                 ↓\n+Phase 5: [17] → [18] → [19] → [20]\n+                 ↓\n+Phase 6: [21] → [22] → [23] → [24]\n+```\n+\n+Steps within a phase are largely sequential. Phases can overlap slightly where independent work exists.\n+\n+## Project Creation Order\n+\n+### Phase 0: Infrastructure Setup (Before Any Implementation)\n+\n+Initialize all empty repositories with infrastructure from riotprompt:\n+\n+```bash\n+# For each empty repo (commands-plan, commands-status, etc.):\n+cd /Users/tobrien/gitw/kjerneverk/REPO_NAME\n+\n+# Copy infrastructure\n+cp -r ../riotprompt/.github .\n+cp ../riotprompt/.gitignore ../riotprompt/.npmrc ../riotprompt/.nvmrc .\n+cp ../riotprompt/tsconfig.json ../riotprompt/eslint.config.mjs ../riotprompt/LICENSE .\n+\n+# Create directories\n+mkdir -p src tests guide\n+\n+# Create vite.config.ts, vitest.config.ts, package.json\n+# Create src/index.ts, guide/index.md, README.md\n+\n+# Initialize and VERIFY\n+npm install\n+npm run build\n+npm run precommit   # ← MUST PASS before committing\n+\n+# Only commit if precommit passes\n+git add . && git commit -m \"Initialize project infrastructure\"\n+git push\n+```\n+\n+**⚠️ DO NOT commit or push until `npm run precommit` passes!**\n+\n+### Phase 1-2: Core Enhancement\n+\n+Enhance existing `riotplan` package only\n+\n+### Phase 3: Command Packages\n+\n+Initialize and implement (repos already created):\n+- `commands-plan/` → `@riotprompt/riotplan-commands-plan`\n+- `commands-status/` → `@riotprompt/riotplan-commands-status`\n+- `commands-step/` → `@riotprompt/riotplan-commands-step` (create repo)\n+- `commands-feedback/` → `@riotprompt/riotplan-commands-feedback`\n+- Refactor `riotplan-cli` to import from above\n+\n+### Phase 4: Dashboard\n+\n+Create and implement:\n+- `dashboard/` → `@riotprompt/riotplan-dashboard` (create repo, web server on port 8090)\n+\n+### Phase 5: Templates & Rendering\n+\n+Initialize and implement (repos already created):\n+- `templates/` → `@riotprompt/riotplan-templates`\n+- `renderer/` → `@riotprompt/riotplan-renderer`\n+- `commands-render/` → `@riotprompt/riotplan-commands-render`\n+\n+### Phase 6: History\n+\n+Initialize and implement (repo already created):\n+- `history/` → `@riotprompt/riotplan-history`\n+\n+---\n+\n+*Execution plan version: 2.0*\n+*Last updated: 2026-01-14*\ndiff --git a/prompts/implement-riotplan/PROPOSED_PROJECTS.md b/prompts/implement-riotplan/PROPOSED_PROJECTS.md\n... Based on comprehensive analysis captured in `RIOTPLAN_ANALYSIS.md`, RiotPlan addresses a core problem: managing complex, multi-step, cross-session work is cognitively demanding. Plans evolve through multiple revisions, spawn new projects, and require persistent tracking across sessions and repositories.\n+\n+## Philosophy\n+\n+> **RiotPlan preserves deliberation, not just decisions.**\n+\n+A plan is not a static document—it's the crystallized result of a process. That process (inception, feedback, revision, debate, execution, reflection) is itself valuable. RiotPlan captures:\n+\n+- What was proposed\n+- What feedback was given\n+- What was changed as a result\n+- Why the change was made\n+- Who was involved\n+\n+## Core Value Proposition\n+\n+| Pain Point | Current State | With RiotPlan |\n+|------------|---------------|---------------|\n+| **Context recovery** | 2+ hours/day reconstructing \"where was I?\" | 10 seconds via `riotplan dashboard` |\n+| **Lost conversations** | Decisions vanish when tools close | Preserved in `feedback/` records |\n+| **Plan relationships** | Held in human memory | Tracked in dependency graph |\n+| **Status tracking** | Manual STATUS.md updates | Auto-updated on step completion |\n+| **Cross-repo coordination** | Mental gymnastics | Single dashboard across all repos |\n+\n+## Technology Stack\n+\n+Following kjerneverk patterns:\n+\n+- **Node.js**: >=24.0.0\n+- **TypeScript**: 5.8.x with ES2024 target\n+- **Build**: Vite 7.x\n+- **Testing**: Vitest\n+- **Linting**: ESLint\n+- **Package Scope**: `@riotprompt/*`\n+- **License**: Apache-2.0\n+- **Module System**: ESM only (`\"type\": \"module\"`)\n+\n+## Success Criteria\n+\n+1. **Zero-Config Start**: `riotplan init my-feature` creates a valid plan immediately\n+2. **Context Recovery**: `riotplan status` tells you exactly where you are\n+3. **Blocker Visibility**: Blocked work is immediately obvious\n+4. **Cross-Plan Awareness**: Dependencies across projects are tracked\n+5. **Human-Readable**: Plans work without riotplan installed\n+6. **Feedback Capture**: Deliberation is preserved as natural byproduct\n+\n+## Scope\n+\n+### In Scope (v1.0)\n+\n+- Core plan management (create, load, validate)\n+- Status tracking (parse/generate STATUS.md)\n+- Step operations (add, remove, block, complete)\n+- Feedback capture (create feedback records)\n+- Dependency management (within-plan, cross-plan)\n+- Multi-plan dashboard\n+- CLI for all operations\n+- Starter templates (feature, bugfix, content, etc.)\n+- Document rendering (PDF, HTML, DOCX)\n+\n+### Out of Scope (v1.0)\n+\n+- Real-time collaborative editing (Git provides collaboration)\n+- Cloud sync (all local)\n+- AI plan generation (RiotPlan manages plans, doesn't create them)\n+- Mobile app\n+- Hosted sharing with URLs\n+- Semantic search / RAG (Phase 4-5)\n+\n+## Existing Foundation\n+\n+The following exist with stub implementations:\n+\n+- `@riotprompt/riotplan` - Core library with types defined\n+- `@riotprompt/riotplan-cli` - CLI with stub commands\n+\n+## Evidence Base\n+\n+This plan is based on:\n+\n+- `RIOTPLAN_ANALYSIS.md` - Comprehensive analysis document (3,400+ lines)\n+- 10 evidence files (`what-happened-*.md`) from real plan executions\n+- 31 feedback records documenting the analysis evolution\n+- External critical review from ChatGPT 5.2 Pro Deep Research\n+\n+## Related Plans\n+\n+- `reverse-engineer-riotplan` - The analysis that produced this implementation plan\n+- `big-splitup` - Example of plan spawning multiple projects\n+- `secure-riotprompt` - Example of cross-project dependencies\n+\n+---\n+\n+*Plan created: 2026-01-14*\n+*Based on: RIOTPLAN_ANALYSIS.md b/prompts/implement-riotplan/implement-riotplan-prompt.md\n@@ -0,0 +1,83 @@\n+# Meta-Prompt: Implement RiotPlan\n+\n+## Context\n+\n+You are implementing RiotPlan—a framework for managing long-lived, stateful AI workflows (plans). This plan was generated from comprehensive analysis documented in `RIOTPLAN_ANALYSIS.md`.\n+\n+## The Problem\n+\n+Managing complex, multi-step, cross-session work is cognitively demanding. Plans evolve through multiple revisions, spawn new projects, and require persistent tracking across sessions and repositories. Most critically: the deliberation, feedback, and reasoning that shapes plans is lost when conversations end.\n+\n+## The Solution\n+\n+RiotPlan captures:\n+- What was proposed\n+- What feedback was given\n+- What was changed as a result\n+- Why the change was made\n+- Who was involved\n+\n+## Technical Requirements\n+\n+- **Node.js**: >=24.0.0\n+- **TypeScript**: 5.8.x with ES2024 target\n+- **Build**: Vite 7.x\n+- **Testing**: Vitest with >80% coverage\n+- **Linting**: ESLint\n+- **Module System**: ESM only (`\"type\": \"module\"`)\n+- **Package Scope**: `@riotprompt/*`\n+\n+## Existing Foundation\n+\n+Two packages already exist with stub implementations:\n+- `@riotprompt/riotplan` - Core library with types\n+- `@riotprompt/riotplan-cli` - CLI with stub commands\n+\n+## Key Principles\n+\n+1. **Feedback capture is day-one**: Not a future feature\n+2. **Progressive enhancement**: Works with existing plans\n+3. **Human-readable**: Plans work without riotplan installed\n+4. **File-based state**: No database, just markdown\n+5. **Git is the collaboration layer**: No built-in real-time sync\n+\n+## Phase Structure\n+\n+| Phase | Steps | Focus |\n+|-------|-------|-------|\n+| Phase 1 | 01-04 | Foundation: Types, loading, creation, feedback |\n+| Phase 2 | 05-08 | Status & Steps: Parsing, generation, operations, validation |\n+| Phase 3 | 09-12 | CLI Foundation: Architecture, plan/status/feedback commands |\n+| Phase 4 | 13-16 | Dependencies & Dashboard: Internal, external, registry, dashboard |\n+| Phase 5 | 17-20 | Templates & Rendering: Templates, commands, renderer, render commands |\n+| Phase 6 | 21-24 | Advanced: History, retrospectives, providers, documentation |\n+\n+## Quality Gates\n+\n+After each step:\n+1. `npm run lint` passes\n+2. `npm run test` with >80% coverage\n+3. `npm run build` produces valid output\n+4. Works with existing plans in kjerneverk\n+\n+## Evidence\n+\n+This plan is based on:\n+- `../reverse-engineer-riotplan/RIOTPLAN_ANALYSIS.md` - Comprehensive analysis (3,400+ lines)\n+- 10 evidence files documenting real plan execution\n+- 31 feedback records documenting the analysis evolution\n+\n+## Success Criteria\n+\n+1. **Zero-Config Start**: `riotplan init my-feature` creates a valid plan\n+2. **Context Recovery**: `riotplan status` shows exactly where you are\n+3. **Blocker Visibility**: Blocked work is immediately obvious\n+4. **Feedback Capture**: Deliberation is preserved automatically\n+5. **Human-Readable**: Plans work without riotplan installed\n+\n+---\n+\n+*For detailed requirements, see RIOTPLAN_ANALYSIS.md*\n+*For step-by-step instructions, see b/prompts/implement-riotplan/plan/01-type-system-enhancement.md\n@@ -0,0 +1,354 @@\n+# Step 01: Type System Enhancement\n+\n+## Objective\n+\n+Extend the existing type definitions in `@riotprompt/riotplan` to support feedback capture, evidence tracking, embedded versioning, and the full plan lifecycle defined in RIOTPLAN_ANALYSIS.md.\n+\n+## Background\n+\n+The existing `src/types.ts` in riotplan provides a solid foundation with types for:\n+- `Plan`, `PlanStep`, `PlanPhase`, `PlanState`\n+- `Blocker`, `Issue`\n+- `StatusDocument`, `ExecutionPlanDocument`\n+- `PLAN_CONVENTIONS`\n+\n+However, the analysis identified additional requirements:\n+- Feedback records for deliberation capture\n+- Evidence tracking for inception phase materials\n+- Revision/version history embedded in plans\n+- Contexts (work, personal, etc.)\n+- Cross-plan relationships and spawning\n+\n+## Tasks\n+\n+### 1. Add Feedback Types\n+\n+```typescript\n+// Feedback record for deliberation capture\n+export interface FeedbackRecord {\n+  /** Unique identifier (e.g., \"001\", \"002\") */\n+  id: string;\n+  \n+  /** Title/subject of the feedback */\n+  title: string;\n+  \n+  /** When created */\n+  createdAt: Date;\n+  \n+  /** Participants in the discussion */\n+  participants: FeedbackParticipant[];\n+  \n+  /** Platform where feedback occurred */\n+  platform: FeedbackPlatform;\n+  \n+  /** Plan version this feedback relates to */\n+  planVersion?: string;\n+  \n+  /** Related file references (file:lines) */\n+  context?: FeedbackContext[];\n+  \n+  /** What was proposed before feedback */\n+  proposed?: string;\n+  \n+  /** The feedback given */\n+  feedback: string;\n+  \n+  /** Discussion/debate content */\n+  discussion?: string;\n+  \n+  /** Resolution/outcome */\n+  resolution?: string;\n+  \n+  /** Changes made as a result */\n+  changes?: string[];\n+  \n+  /** Open questions remaining */\n+  openQuestions?: string[];\n+  \n+  /** Filename (e.g., \"001-initial-review.md\") */\n+  filename: string;\n+}\n+\n+export interface FeedbackParticipant {\n+  name: string;\n+  type: 'human' | 'ai';\n+  model?: string; // e.g., \"claude-sonnet-4-20250514\"\n+}\n+\n+export type FeedbackPlatform = \n+  | 'cursor'\n+  | 'chatgpt'\n+  | 'slack'\n+  | 'email'\n+  | 'meeting'\n+  | 'voice'\n+  | 'document'\n+  | 'other';\n+\n+export interface FeedbackContext {\n+  file: string;\n+  startLine?: number;\n+  endLine?: number;\n+  content?: string;\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 2. Add Evidence Types\n+\n+```typescript\n+// Evidence for inception phase\n+export interface EvidenceRecord {\n+  /** Unique identifier */\n+  id: string;\n+  \n+  /** Evidence type */\n+  type: EvidenceType;\n+  \n+  /** Title/description */\n+  title: string;\n+  \n+  /** When gathered */\n+  createdAt: Date;\n+  \n+  /** Source of evidence */\n+  source?: string;\n+  \n+  /** Filename */\n+  filename: string;\n+  \n+  /** Summary of key findings */\n+  summary?: string;\n+  \n+  /** Tags for categorization */\n+  tags?: string[];\n+}\n+\n+export type EvidenceType = \n+  | 'case-study'      // what-happened-in-*.md\n+  | 'research'        // research-*.md\n+  | 'analysis'        // analysis-*.md\n+  | 'example'         // example-*.md\n+  | 'external-review' // External AI review\n+  | 'reference';      // Reference material\n+```\n+\n+### 3. Add Revision/History Types\n+\n+```typescript\n+// Embedded version tracking\n+export interface PlanRevision {\n+  /** Version string (e.g., \"0.1\", \"0.2\") */\n+  version: string;\n+  \n+  /** When this revision was created */\n+  createdAt: Date;\n+  \n+  /** Commit message/description */\n+  message?: string;\n+  \n+  /** Author */\n+  author?: string;\n+  \n+  /** Feedback record that triggered this revision */\n+  feedbackId?: string;\n+}\n+\n+export interface PlanHistory {\n+  /** All revisions in order */\n+  revisions: PlanRevision[];\n+  \n+  /** Current version */\n+  currentVersion: string;\n+  \n+  /** Milestones (explicit cleanup points) */\n+  milestones?: PlanMilestone[];\n+}\n+\n+export interface PlanMilestone {\n+  name: string;\n+  version: string;\n+  createdAt: Date;\n+  description?: string;\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 4. Add Context Types\n+\n+```typescript\n+// Plan contexts (work, personal, etc.)\n+export interface PlanContext {\n+  /** Context identifier */\n+  id: string;\n+  \n+  /** Display name */\n+  name: string;\n+  \n+  /** Parent context (for hierarchy) */\n+  parent?: string;\n+  \n+  /** Default for this directory? */\n+  isDefault?: boolean;\n+}\n+\n+export type ContextId = string; // e.g., \"work\", \"personal\", \"work/kjerneverk\"\n+```\n+\n+### 5. Add Cross-Plan Relationship Types\n+\n+```typescript\n+// Cross-plan dependencies and spawning\n+export interface PlanRelationship {\n+  /** Type of relationship */\n+  type: RelationshipType;\n+  \n+  /** Related plan path */\n+  planPath: string;\n+  \n+  /** Specific step(s) involved */\n+  steps?: number[];\n+  \n+  /** Reason for relationship */\n+  reason?: string;\n+  \n+  /** When established */\n+  createdAt: Date;\n+}\n+\n+export type RelationshipType = \n+  | 'spawned-from'   // This plan was spawned from another\n+  | 'spawned'        // This plan spawned another\n+  | 'blocks'         // This plan blocks another\n+  | 'blocked-by'     // This plan is blocked by another\n+  | 'related';       // General relationship\n+```\n+\n+### 6. Extend Existing Types\n+\n+Update `Plan` interface:\n+\n+```typescript\n+export interface Plan {\n+  // ... existing fields ...\n+  \n+  /** Feedback records */\n+  feedback?: FeedbackRecord[];\n+  \n+  /** Evidence files */\n+  evidence?: EvidenceRecord[];\n+  \n+  /** Revision history */\n+  history?: PlanHistory;\n+  \n+  /** Context assignment */\n+  context?: ContextId;\n+  \n+  /** Relationships to other plans */\n+  relationships?: PlanRelationship[];\n+}\n+```\n+\n+Update `PlanFiles` interface:\n+\n+```typescript\n+export interface PlanFiles {\n+  // ... existing fields ...\n+  \n+  /** Feedback directory files */\n+  feedbackDir?: string;\n+  feedbackFiles?: string[];\n+  \n+  /** Evidence directory files */\n+  evidenceDir?: string;\n+  evidenceFiles?: string[];\n+  \n+  /** History directory */\n+  historyDir?: string;\n+  \n+  /** CHANGELOG.md */\n+  changelog?: string;\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 7. Add Plan Status Constants\n+\n+```typescript\n+// Expand PLAN_CONVENTIONS\n+export const PLAN_CONVENTIONS = {\n+  // ... existing ...\n+  \n+  standardFiles: {\n+    // ... existing ...\n+    changelog: \"CHANGELOG.md\",\n+  },\n+  \n+  standardDirs: {\n+    // ... existing ...\n+    feedback: \"feedback\",\n+    evidence: \"evidence\",\n+    history: \".history\",\n+  },\n+  \n+  feedbackPattern: /^(\\d{3})-(.+)\\.md$/,\n+  evidencePattern: /^(what-happened-|research-|analysis-|example-)(.+)\\.md$/,\n+} as const;\n+```\n+\n+## Acceptance Criteria\n+\n+- [ ] All new types are defined in `src/types.ts`\n+- [ ] Types are exported from `src/index.ts`\n+- [ ] TypeScript compiles without errors\n+- [ ] JSDoc comments on all public types\n+- [ ] Types align with RIOTPLAN_ANALYSIS.md requirements (R9, R10, R14, R19)\n+- [ ] **`npm run precommit` passes** ← Definition of Done\n+\n+## Testing\n+\n+```typescript\n+// tests/types.test.ts\n+import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';\n+import type { \n+  FeedbackRecord, \n+  EvidenceRecord, \n+  PlanRevision,\n+  PlanRelationship \n+} from '../src/types';\n+\n+describe('Extended Types', () => {\n+  it('should allow creating a FeedbackRecord', () => {\n+    const feedback: FeedbackRecord = {\n+      id: '001',\n+      title: 'Initial Review',\n+      createdAt: new Date(),\n+      participants: [\n+        { name: 'Tim', type: 'human' },\n+        { name: 'Claude', type: 'ai', model: 'claude-sonnet-4-20250514' }\n+      ],\n+      platform: 'cursor',\n+      feedback: 'Consider adding feedback capture',\n+      filename: '001-initial-review.md'\n+    };\n+    expect(feedback.id).toBe('001');\n+  });\n+  \n+  // ... more tests\n+});\n+```\n+\n+## Files Changed\n+\n+- `riotplan/src/types.ts` - Extended with new types\n+- `riotplan/src/index.ts` - Export new types\n+- `riotplan/tests/types.test.ts` - Type tests\n+\n+## Notes\n+\n+- Keep backward compatibility with existing types\n+- Consider creating separate files if types.ts grows too large\n+- These types will be used by feedback capture (Step 04) and status parsing (Step 05)\n+\n+## References\n+\n+- RIOTPLAN_ANALYSIS.md: Part 1 (Plan Structure), R9, R10, R14, R19\n+- Existing `riotplan/src/types.ts`\n+\ndiff --git a/prompts/implement-riotplan/plan/02-plan-loading.md b/prompts/implement-riotplan/plan/02-plan-loading.md\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..b8ebace\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/prompts/implement-riotplan/plan/02-plan-loading.md\n@@ -0,0 +1,406 @@\n+# Step 02: Plan Loading\n+\n+## Objective\n+\n+Implement `loadPlan()` function to parse existing plan directories into the `Plan` data structure, including all standard files (SUMMARY.md, EXECUTION_PLAN.md, STATUS.md), step files, feedback records, and evidence.\n+\n+## Background\n+\n+Plans exist as directories with markdown files. The `loadPlan()` function must:\n+1. Discover and validate plan structure\n+2. Parse all standard files\n+3. Load step files in order\n+4. Load feedback records if present\n+5. Load evidence files if present\n+6. Return a complete `Plan` object\n+\n+The stub already exists in `src/index.ts`. This step implements it fully.\n+\n+## Tasks\n+\n+### 1. Create Plan Loader Module\n+\n+Create `src/plan/loader.ts`:\n+\n+```typescript\n+import { readdir, readFile, stat } from 'node:fs/promises';\n+import { join, basename, extname } from 'node:path';\n+import type { \n+  Plan, \n+  PlanFiles, \n+  PlanMetadata, \n+  PlanStep,\n+  PlanState,\n+  FeedbackRecord,\n+  EvidenceRecord \n+} from '../types.js';\n+import { PLAN_CONVENTIONS } from '../types.js';\n+\n+export interface LoadPlanOptions {\n+  /** Include feedback records */\n+  includeFeedback?: boolean;\n+  /** Include evidence files */\n+  includeEvidence?: boolean;\n+  /** Parse STATUS.md for state */\n+  parseStatus?: boolean;\n+}\n+\n+export async function loadPlan(\n+  path: string, \n+  options: LoadPlanOptions = {}\n+): Promise<Plan> {\n+  // Implementation\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 2. Implement Directory Discovery\n+\n+```typescript\n+async function discoverPlanFiles(planPath: string): Promise<PlanFiles> {\n+  const files: PlanFiles = {\n+    steps: [],\n+    subdirectories: []\n+  };\n+  \n+  const entries = await readdir(planPath, { withFileTypes: true });\n+  \n+  for (const entry of entries) {\n+    if (entry.isDirectory()) {\n+      // Check standard directories\n+      if (entry.name === PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardDirs.plan) {\n+        files.steps = await discoverStepFiles(join(planPath, entry.name));\n+      } else if (entry.name === PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardDirs.feedback) {\n+        files.feedbackDir = entry.name;\n+        files.feedbackFiles = await discoverFeedbackFiles(join(planPath, entry.name));\n+      }\n+      // ... etc\n+    } else if (entry.isFile()) {\n+      // Check standard files\n+      const name = entry.name;\n+      if (name === PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardFiles.summary) {\n+        files.summary = name;\n+      } else if (name === PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardFiles.status) {\n+        files.status = name;\n+      }\n+      // ... etc\n+    }\n+  }\n+  \n+  return files;\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 3. Implement Step File Loading\n+\n+```typescript\n+async function discoverStepFiles(planDir: string): Promise<string[]> {\n+  const entries = await readdir(planDir);\n+  const stepFiles: string[] = [];\n+  \n+  for (const entry of entries) {\n+    if (PLAN_CONVENTIONS.stepPattern.test(entry)) {\n+      stepFiles.push(entry);\n+    }\n+  }\n+  \n+  // Sort by step number\n+  return stepFiles.sort((a, b) => {\n+    const numA = parseInt(a.match(PLAN_CONVENTIONS.stepPattern)![1]);\n+    const numB = parseInt(b.match(PLAN_CONVENTIONS.stepPattern)![1]);\n+    return numA - numB;\n+  });\n+}\n+\n+async function loadSteps(\n+  planPath: string, \n+  stepFiles: string[]\n+): Promise<PlanStep[]> {\n+  const steps: PlanStep[] = [];\n+  \n+  for (const filename of stepFiles) {\n+    const match = filename.match(PLAN_CONVENTIONS.stepPattern);\n+    if (!match) continue;\n+    \n+    const [, numberStr, code] = match;\n+    const number = parseInt(numberStr);\n+    const filePath = join(planPath, PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardDirs.plan, filename);\n+    const content = await readFile(filePath, 'utf-8');\n+    \n+    steps.push({\n+      number,\n+      code,\n+      filename,\n+      title: extractTitle(content) || code,\n+      description: extractDescription(content),\n+      status: 'pending', // Default, may be overridden by STATUS.md\n+      filePath\n+    });\n+  }\n+  \n+  return steps;\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 4. Implement Metadata Extraction\n+\n+```typescript\n+async function extractMetadata(\n+  planPath: string, \n+  files: PlanFiles\n+): Promise<PlanMetadata> {\n+  const code = basename(planPath);\n+  let name = code;\n+  let description: string | undefined;\n+  \n+  // Try to extract from SUMMARY.md\n+  if (files.summary) {\n+    const content = await readFile(join(planPath, files.summary), 'utf-8');\n+    name = extractTitle(content) || code;\n+    description = extractFirstParagraph(content);\n+  }\n+  \n+  return {\n+    code,\n+    name,\n+    description,\n+    path: planPath\n+  };\n+}\n+\n+function extractTitle(markdown: string): string | null {\n+  const match = markdown.match(/^#\\s+(.+)$/m);\n+  return match ? match[1].trim() : null;\n+}\n+\n+function extractFirstParagraph(markdown: string): string | undefined {\n+  // Skip title, find first paragraph\n+  const lines = markdown.split('\\n');\n+  let foundTitle = false;\n+  let paragraph = '';\n+  \n+  for (const line of lines) {\n+    if (line.startsWith('#')) {\n+      foundTitle = true;\n+      continue;\n+    }\n+    if (foundTitle && line.trim()) {\n+      if (line.startsWith('#') || line.startsWith('-') || line.startsWith('|')) {\n+        break; // Hit another section\n+      }\n+      paragraph += line + ' ';\n+    } else if (foundTitle && paragraph && !line.trim()) {\n+      break; // End of paragraph\n+    }\n+  }\n+  \n+  return paragraph.trim() || undefined;\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 5. Implement Feedback Loading\n+\n+```typescript\n+async function loadFeedbackRecords(\n+  feedbackPath: string\n+): Promise<FeedbackRecord[]> {\n+  const files = await readdir(feedbackPath);\n+  const records: FeedbackRecord[] = [];\n+  \n+  for (const file of files) {\n+    if (!PLAN_CONVENTIONS.feedbackPattern.test(file)) continue;\n+    \n+    const content = await readFile(join(feedbackPath, file), 'utf-8');\n+    const record = parseFeedbackFile(file, content);\n+    if (record) records.push(record);\n+  }\n+  \n+  return records.sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id));\n+}\n+\n+function parseFeedbackFile(\n+  filename: string, \n+  content: string\n+): FeedbackRecord | null {\n+  const match = filename.match(PLAN_CONVENTIONS.feedbackPattern);\n+  if (!match) return null;\n+  \n+  const [, id, titleSlug] = match;\n+  \n+  // Parse frontmatter and content\n+  const { frontmatter, body } = parseFrontmatter(content);\n+  \n+  return {\n+    id,\n+    title: frontmatter.title || titleSlug.replace(/-/g, ' '),\n+    createdAt: frontmatter.date ? new Date(frontmatter.date) : new Date(),\n+    participants: frontmatter.participants || [],\n+    platform: frontmatter.platform || 'other',\n+    feedback: body,\n+    filename\n+  };\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 6. Assemble Complete loadPlan Function\n+\n+```typescript\n+export async function loadPlan(\n+  path: string, \n+  options: LoadPlanOptions = {}\n+): Promise<Plan> {\n+  const { \n+    includeFeedback = true, \n+    includeEvidence = true,\n+    parseStatus = true \n+  } = options;\n+  \n+  // Verify path exists and is directory\n+  const pathStat = await stat(path);\n+  if (!pathStat.isDirectory()) {\n+    throw new Error(`Plan path is not a directory: ${path}`);\n+  }\n+  \n+  // Discover files\n+  const files = await discoverPlanFiles(path);\n+  \n+  // Extract metadata\n+  const metadata = await extractMetadata(path, files);\n+  \n+  // Load steps\n+  const steps = files.steps.length > 0 \n+    ? await loadSteps(path, files.steps)\n+    : [];\n+  \n+  // Initialize state\n+  let state: PlanState = {\n+    status: 'pending',\n+    lastUpdatedAt: new Date(),\n+    blockers: [],\n+    issues: [],\n+    progress: 0\n+  };\n+  \n+  // Parse STATUS.md if present and requested\n+  if (parseStatus && files.status) {\n+    const statusContent = await readFile(join(path, files.status), 'utf-8');\n+    state = parseStatusDocument(statusContent, steps);\n+  }\n+  \n+  // Load feedback if requested\n+  const feedback = includeFeedback && files.feedbackDir\n+    ? await loadFeedbackRecords(join(path, files.feedbackDir))\n+    : undefined;\n+  \n+  // Load evidence if requested\n+  const evidence = includeEvidence && files.evidenceDir\n+    ? await loadEvidenceRecords(join(path, files.evidenceDir))\n+    : undefined;\n+  \n+  return {\n+    metadata,\n+    files,\n+    steps,\n+    state,\n+    feedback,\n+    evidence\n+  };\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 7. Update Index Exports\n+\n+In `src/index.ts`:\n+\n+```typescript\n+export { loadPlan, type LoadPlanOptions } from './plan/loader.js';\n+```\n+\n+## Acceptance Criteria\n+\n+- [ ] `loadPlan(path)` returns valid Plan object for existing plans\n+- [ ] Handles plans with and without `plan/` subdirectory\n+- [ ] Correctly parses step file numbering and ordering\n+- [ ] Extracts metadata from SUMMARY.md\n+- [ ] Loads feedback records when `feedback/` exists\n+- [ ] Loads evidence records when `evidence/` exists\n+- [ ] Throws clear errors for invalid plan structures\n+- [ ] **`npm run precommit` passes** ← Definition of Done\n+\n+## Testing\n+\n+```typescript\n+// tests/loader.test.ts\n+import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';\n+import { loadPlan } from '../src/index';\n+import { join } from 'node:path';\n+\n+describe('loadPlan', () => {\n+  const fixturesPath = join(__dirname, 'fixtures');\n+  \n+  it('should load a valid plan', async () => {\n+    const plan = await loadPlan(join(fixturesPath, 'valid-plan'));\n+    expect(plan.metadata.code).toBe('valid-plan');\n+    expect(plan.steps.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n+  });\n+  \n+  it('should throw for non-existent path', async () => {\n+    await expect(loadPlan('/nonexistent')).rejects.toThrow();\n+  });\n+  \n+  it('should load feedback records', async () => {\n+    const plan = await loadPlan(join(fixturesPath, 'plan-with-feedback'));\n+    expect(plan.feedback).toBeDefined();\n+    expect(plan.feedback!.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n+  });\n+  \n+  it('should handle plan without feedback dir', async () => {\n+    const plan = await loadPlan(join(fixturesPath, 'minimal-plan'));\n+    expect(plan.feedback).toBeUndefined();\n+  });\n+});\n+```\n+\n+### Test Fixtures\n+\n+Create test fixtures:\n+\n+```\n+tests/fixtures/\n+├── valid-plan/\n+│   ├── SUMMARY.md\n+│   ├── STATUS.md\n+│   └── plan/\n+│       ├── 01-first-step.md\n+│       └── 02-second-step.md\n+├── plan-with-feedback/\n+│   ├── SUMMARY.md\n+│   ├── plan/\n+│   │   └── 01-step.md\n+│   └── feedback/\n+│       └── 001-initial-review.md\n+└── minimal-plan/\n+    └── SUMMARY.md\n+```\n+\n+## Files Changed\n+\n+- `riotplan/src/plan/loader.ts` - New file\n+- `riotplan/src/index.ts` - Export loadPlan\n+- `riotplan/tests/loader.test.ts` - Tests\n+- `riotplan/tests/fixtures/` - Test plans\n+\n+## Notes\n+\n+- Uses native Node.js `fs/promises` (no external dependencies)\n+- Backward compatible with existing kjerneverk plans\n+- STATUS.md parsing is basic here; full parsing in Step 05\n+\n+##
