--git a/prompts/implement-riotplan/plan/03-plan-creation.md b/prompts/implement-riotplan/plan/03-plan-creation.md\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..ac3275f\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/prompts/implement-riotplan/plan/03-plan-creation.md\n@@ -0,0 +1,514 @@\n+# Step 03: Plan Creation\n+\n+## Objective\n+\n+Implement `createPlan()` function to scaffold new plan directories with all standard files, proper structure, and optionally from templates.\n+\n+## Background\n+\n+Users need to create new plans quickly. The `createPlan()` function must:\n+1. Create directory structure\n+2. Generate standard files (SUMMARY.md, EXECUTION_PLAN.md, STATUS.md)\n+3. Create initial step files\n+4. Initialize feedback/ and evidence/ directories\n+5. Support template-based creation (basic support; full templates in Phase 5)\n+\n+## Tasks\n+\n+### 1. Create Plan Creator Module\n+\n+Create `src/plan/creator.ts`:\n+\n+```typescript\n+import { mkdir, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';\n+import { join } from 'node:path';\n+import type { Plan, PlanStep } from '../types.js';\n+import { PLAN_CONVENTIONS } from '../types.js';\n+\n+export interface CreatePlanConfig {\n+  /** Plan code/slug (directory name) */\n+  code: string;\n+  \n+  /** Human-readable name */\n+  name: string;\n+  \n+  /** Base path for plan (plan created as subdirectory) */\n+  basePath: string;\n+  \n+  /** Plan description */\n+  description?: string;\n+  \n+  /** Initial steps */\n+  steps?: Array<{ title: string; description?: string }>;\n+  \n+  /** Context assignment */\n+  context?: string;\n+  \n+  /** Author name */\n+  author?: string;\n+  \n+  /** Create feedback directory */\n+  createFeedbackDir?: boolean;\n+  \n+  /** Create evidence directory */\n+  createEvidenceDir?: boolean;\n+  \n+  /** Template to use (basic support) */\n+  template?: 'blank' | 'default';\n+}\n+\n+export interface CreatePlanResult {\n+  /** Created plan */\n+  plan: Plan;\n+  \n+  /** Path where plan was created */\n+  path: string;\n+  \n+  /** Files that were created */\n+  filesCreated: string[];\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 2. Implement Directory Creation\n+\n+```typescript\n+async function createPlanDirectories(\n+  planPath: string,\n+  options: { feedback?: boolean; evidence?: boolean }\n+): Promise<string[]> {\n+  const dirs: string[] = [];\n+  \n+  // Create main directory\n+  await mkdir(planPath, { recursive: true });\n+  dirs.push(planPath);\n+  \n+  // Create plan/ subdirectory for steps\n+  const planDir = join(planPath, PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardDirs.plan);\n+  await mkdir(planDir, { recursive: true });\n+  dirs.push(planDir);\n+  \n+  // Create feedback/ if requested\n+  if (options.feedback) {\n+    const feedbackDir = join(planPath, PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardDirs.feedback);\n+    await mkdir(feedbackDir, { recursive: true });\n+    dirs.push(feedbackDir);\n+  }\n+  \n+  // Create evidence/ if requested\n+  if (options.evidence) {\n+    const evidenceDir = join(planPath, PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardDirs.evidence);\n+    await mkdir(evidenceDir, { recursive: true });\n+    dirs.push(evidenceDir);\n+  }\n+  \n+  return dirs;\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 3. Implement File Generators\n+\n+```typescript\n+function generateSummaryMd(config: CreatePlanConfig): string {\n+  const date = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];\n+  \n+  return `# ${config.name}\n+\n+## Overview\n+\n+${config.description || 'Description of this plan.'}\n+\n+## Goals\n+\n+1. Goal 1\n+2. Goal 2\n+3. Goal 3\n+\n+## Scope\n+\n+### In Scope\n+\n+- Item 1\n+- Item 2\n+\n+### Out of Scope\n+\n+- Item 1\n+\n+## Success Criteria\n+\n+- [ ] Criterion 1\n+- [ ] Criterion 2\n+\n+---\n+\n+*Plan created: ${date}*\n+${config.author ? `*Author: ${config.author}*` : ''}\n+`;\n+}\n+\n+function generateExecutionPlanMd(config: CreatePlanConfig): string {\n+  const steps = config.steps || [\n+    { title: 'Setup', description: 'Initial setup and prerequisites' },\n+    { title: 'Implementation', description: 'Core implementation work' },\n+    { title: 'Testing', description: 'Verify everything works' }\n+  ];\n+  \n+  let stepsSection = '';\n+  steps.forEach((step, i) => {\n+    const num = String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0');\n+    stepsSection += `| ${num} | ${step.title} | ${step.description || ''} |\\n`;\n+  });\n+  \n+  return `# Execution Plan: ${config.name}\n+\n+## Strategy\n+\n+Describe the overall approach and strategy for this plan.\n+\n+## Prerequisites\n+\n+- [ ] Prerequisite 1\n+- [ ] Prerequisite 2\n+\n+## Steps\n+\n+| Step | Name | Description |\n+|------|------|-------------|\n+${stepsSection}\n+\n+## Quality Gates\n+\n+After each step:\n+- [ ] Tests pass\n+- [ ] Lint passes\n+- [ ] Code review complete\n+\n+## Notes\n+\n+Additional notes about the execution approach.\n+\n+---\n+\n+*Last updated: ${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}*\n+`;\n+}\n+\n+function generateStatusMd(config: CreatePlanConfig): string {\n+  const date = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];\n+  const steps = config.steps || [\n+    { title: 'Setup' },\n+    { title: 'Implementation' },\n+    { title: 'Testing' }\n+  ];\n+  \n+  let stepsTable = '';\n+  steps.forEach((step, i) => {\n+    const num = String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0');\n+    stepsTable += `| ${num} | ${step.title} | ⬜ | - | - | |\\n`;\n+  });\n+  \n+  return `# ${config.name} Status\n+\n+## Current State\n+\n+| Field | Value |\n+|-------|-------|\n+| **Status** | ⬜ PLANNING |\n+| **Current Step** | - |\n+| **Last Completed** | - |\n+| **Started** | - |\n+| **Last Updated** | ${date} |\n+| **Progress** | 0% (0/${steps.length} steps) |\n+\n+## Step Progress\n+\n+| Step | Name | Status | Started | Completed | Notes |\n+|------|------|--------|---------|-----------|-------|\n+${stepsTable}\n+\n+## Blockers\n+\n+None currently.\n+\n+## Issues\n+\n+None currently.\n+\n+## Notes\n+\n+---\n+\n+*Last updated: ${date}*\n+`;\n+}\n+\n+function generateStepMd(\n+  stepNum: number, \n+  title: string, \n+  description?: string\n+): string {\n+  const num = String(stepNum).padStart(2, '0');\n+  \n+  return `# Step ${num}: ${title}\n+\n+## Objective\n+\n+${description || 'Describe the objective of this step.'}\n+\n+## Background\n+\n+Provide context and rationale for this step.\n+\n+## Tasks\n+\n+### 1. Task 1\n+\n+Description of task 1.\n+\n+### 2. Task 2\n+\n+Description of task 2.\n+\n+## Acceptance Criteria\n+\n+- [ ] Criterion 1\n+- [ ] Criterion 2\n+\n+## Testing\n+\n+Describe how to verify this step is complete.\n+\n+## Files Changed\n+\n+- File 1\n+- File 2\n+\n+## Notes\n+\n+Additional notes for this step.\n+`;\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 4. Implement createPlan Function\n+\n+```typescript\n+export async function createPlan(\n+  config: CreatePlanConfig\n+): Promise<CreatePlanResult> {\n+  const planPath = join(config.basePath, config.code);\n+  const filesCreated: string[] = [];\n+  \n+  // Validate code\n+  if (!/^[a-z0-9-]+$/.test(config.code)) {\n+    throw new Error(\n+      `Invalid plan code: ${config.code}. Use lowercase letters, numbers, and hyphens only.`\n+    );\n+  }\n+  \n+  // Create directories\n+  await createPlanDirectories(planPath, {\n+    feedback: config.createFeedbackDir ?? true,\n+    evidence: config.createEvidenceDir ?? true\n+  });\n+  \n+  // Generate and write SUMMARY.md\n+  const summaryPath = join(planPath, PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardFiles.summary);\n+  await writeFile(summaryPath, generateSummaryMd(config));\n+  filesCreated.push(summaryPath);\n+  \n+  // Generate and write EXECUTION_PLAN.md\n+  const execPath = join(planPath, PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardFiles.executionPlan);\n+  await writeFile(execPath, generateExecutionPlanMd(config));\n+  filesCreated.push(execPath);\n+  \n+  // Generate and write STATUS.md\n+  const statusPath = join(planPath, PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardFiles.status);\n+  await writeFile(statusPath, generateStatusMd(config));\n+  filesCreated.push(statusPath);\n+  \n+  // Create step files\n+  const steps = config.steps || [\n+    { title: 'Setup', description: 'Initial setup and prerequisites' },\n+    { title: 'Implementation', description: 'Core implementation work' },\n+    { title: 'Testing', description: 'Verify everything works' }\n+  ];\n+  \n+  const createdSteps: PlanStep[] = [];\n+  \n+  for (let i = 0; i < steps.length; i++) {\n+    const step = steps[i];\n+    const stepNum = i + 1;\n+    const code = step.title.toLowerCase().replace(/\\s+/g, '-');\n+    const filename = `${String(stepNum).padStart(2, '0')}-${code}.md`;\n+    const stepPath = join(planPath, PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardDirs.plan, filename);\n+    \n+    await writeFile(stepPath, generateStepMd(stepNum, step.title, step.description));\n+    filesCreated.push(stepPath);\n+    \n+    createdSteps.push({\n+      number: stepNum,\n+      code,\n+      filename,\n+      title: step.title,\n+      description: step.description,\n+      status: 'pending',\n+      filePath: stepPath\n+    });\n+  }\n+  \n+  // Construct Plan object\n+  const plan: Plan = {\n+    metadata: {\n+      code: config.code,\n+      name: config.name,\n+      description: config.description,\n+      author: config.author,\n+      path: planPath,\n+      createdAt: new Date()\n+    },\n+    files: {\n+      summary: PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardFiles.summary,\n+      status: PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardFiles.status,\n+      executionPlan: PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardFiles.executionPlan,\n+      steps: createdSteps.map(s => s.filename),\n+      subdirectories: [PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardDirs.plan],\n+      feedbackDir: config.createFeedbackDir ? PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardDirs.feedback : undefined,\n+      evidenceDir: config.createEvidenceDir ? PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardDirs.evidence : undefined\n+    },\n+    steps: createdSteps,\n+    state: {\n+      status: 'pending',\n+      lastUpdatedAt: new Date(),\n+      blockers: [],\n+      issues: [],\n+      progress: 0\n+    },\n+    context: config.context\n+  };\n+  \n+  return { plan, path: planPath, filesCreated };\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 5. Update Index Exports\n+\n+In `src/index.ts`:\n+\n+```typescript\n+export { \n+  createPlan, \n+  type CreatePlanConfig, \n+  type CreatePlanResult \n+} from './plan/creator.js';\n+```\n+\n+## Acceptance Criteria\n+\n+- [ ] `createPlan()` creates valid directory structure\n+- [ ] All standard files are generated correctly\n+- [ ] Step files are numbered and named correctly\n+- [ ] feedback/ and evidence/ directories created when requested\n+- [ ] Generated plan can be loaded with `loadPlan()`\n+- [ ] Clear error messages for invalid inputs\n+- [ ] **`npm run precommit` passes** ← Definition of Done\n+\n+## Testing\n+\n+```typescript\n+// tests/creator.test.ts\n+import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from 'vitest';\n+import { createPlan, loadPlan } from '../src/index';\n+import { rm, access } from 'node:fs/promises';\n+import { join } from 'node:path';\n+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';\n+\n+describe('createPlan', () => {\n+  let testDir: string;\n+  \n+  beforeEach(async () => {\n+    testDir = join(tmpdir(), `riotplan-test-${Date.now()}`);\n+  });\n+  \n+  afterEach(async () => {\n+    try {\n+      await rm(testDir, { recursive: true });\n+    } catch {}\n+  });\n+  \n+  it('should create a valid plan structure', async () => {\n+    const result = await createPlan({\n+      code: 'test-plan',\n+      name: 'Test Plan',\n+      basePath: testDir,\n+      description: 'A test plan'\n+    });\n+    \n+    expect(result.plan.metadata.code).toBe('test-plan');\n+    expect(result.filesCreated.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n+    \n+    // Verify files exist\n+    for (const file of result.filesCreated) {\n+      await expect(access(file)).resolves.not.toThrow();\n+    }\n+  });\n+  \n+  it('should create custom steps', async () => {\n+    const result = await createPlan({\n+      code: 'custom-steps',\n+      name: 'Custom Steps Plan',\n+      basePath: testDir,\n+      steps: [\n+        { title: 'First', description: 'Do first thing' },\n+        { title: 'Second', description: 'Do second thing' }\n+      ]\n+    });\n+    \n+    expect(result.plan.steps.length).toBe(2);\n+    expect(result.plan.steps[0].title).toBe('First');\n+    expect(result.plan.steps[1].title).toBe('Second');\n+  });\n+  \n+  it('should be loadable after creation', async () => {\n+    const result = await createPlan({\n+      code: 'loadable-plan',\n+      name: 'Loadable Plan',\n+      basePath: testDir\n+    });\n+    \n+    const loaded = await loadPlan(result.path);\n+    expect(loaded.metadata.code).toBe('loadable-plan');\n+  });\n+  \n+  it('should reject invalid codes', async () => {\n+    await expect(createPlan({\n+      code: 'Invalid Code!',\n+      name: 'Test',\n+      basePath: testDir\n+    })).rejects.toThrow(/Invalid plan code/);\n+  });\n+});\n+```\n+\n+## Files Changed\n+\n+- `riotplan/src/plan/creator.ts` - New file\n+- `riotplan/src/index.ts` b/prompts/implement-riotplan/plan/04-feedback-capture.md\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..f02fd74\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/prompts/implement-riotplan/plan/04-feedback-capture.md\n@@ -0,0 +1,523 @@\n+# Step 04: Feedback Capture\n+\n+## Objective\n+\n+Implement the feedback capture system—the philosophical foundation of RiotPlan. Feedback records preserve deliberation, not just decisions. This step enables creating, listing, and managing feedback records.\n+\n+## Background\n+\n+From RIOTPLAN_ANALYSIS.md:\n+\n+> **RiotPlan preserves deliberation, not just decisions.**\n+\n+Feedback capture is a **Phase 1 feature, not Phase 4**, because:\n+1. Every plan revision involves deliberation worth capturing\n+2. Without it from the start, early deliberation is lost\n+3. It's the philosophical foundation of the tool\n+4. It can be implemented simply (markdown files) before complex features\n+\n+Feedback records capture:\n+- What was proposed\n+- What feedback was given\n+- What was changed as a result\n+- Why the change was made\n+- Who was involved\n+\n+## Tasks\n+\n+### 1. Create Feedback Module\n+\n+Create `src/feedback/index.ts`:\n+\n+```typescript\n+import { readdir, readFile, writeFile, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';\n+import { join, basename } from 'node:path';\n+import type { \n+  Plan, \n+  FeedbackRecord, \n+  FeedbackParticipant, \n+  FeedbackPlatform,\n+  FeedbackContext \n+} from '../types.js';\n+import { PLAN_CONVENTIONS } from '../types.js';\n+\n+export interface CreateFeedbackOptions {\n+  /** Feedback title */\n+  title: string;\n+  \n+  /** The feedback content */\n+  feedback: string;\n+  \n+  /** Participants */\n+  participants?: FeedbackParticipant[];\n+  \n+  /** Platform where feedback occurred */\n+  platform?: FeedbackPlatform;\n+  \n+  /** What was proposed before feedback */\n+  proposed?: string;\n+  \n+  /** Discussion/debate content */\n+  discussion?: string;\n+  \n+  /** Resolution/outcome */\n+  resolution?: string;\n+  \n+  /** Changes made as a result */\n+  changes?: string[];\n+  \n+  /** Context references (file:lines) */\n+  context?: FeedbackContext[];\n+  \n+  /** Plan version this relates to */\n+  planVersion?: string;\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 2. Implement Feedback ID Generation\n+\n+```typescript\n+async function getNextFeedbackId(feedbackDir: string): Promise<string> {\n+  try {\n+    const files = await readdir(feedbackDir);\n+    let maxId = 0;\n+    \n+    for (const file of files) {\n+      const match = file.match(PLAN_CONVENTIONS.feedbackPattern);\n+      if (match) {\n+        const id = parseInt(match[1]);\n+        if (id > maxId) maxId = id;\n+      }\n+    }\n+    \n+    return String(maxId + 1).padStart(3, '0');\n+  } catch {\n+    // Directory doesn't exist yet\n+    return '001';\n+  }\n+}\n+\n+function slugify(title: string): string {\n+  return title\n+    .toLowerCase()\n+    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')\n+    .replace(/^-|-$/g, '')\n+    .substring(0, 50);\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 3. Implement Feedback File Generation\n+\n+```typescript\n+function generateFeedbackMd(\n+  id: string,\n+  options: CreateFeedbackOptions\n+): string {\n+  const date = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];\n+  \n+  let content = `# Feedback: ${options.title}\n+\n+## Context\n+\n+- **Date**: ${date}\n+`;\n+\n+  // Add participants\n+  if (options.participants && options.participants.length > 0) {\n+    const participantStr = options.participants\n+      .map(p => `${p.name} (${p.type}${p.model ? `, ${p.model}` : ''})`)\n+      .join(', ');\n+    content += `- **Participants**: ${participantStr}\\n`;\n+  }\n+  \n+  // Add platform\n+  if (options.platform) {\n+    content += `- **Platform**: ${options.platform}\\n`;\n+  }\n+  \n+  // Add plan version\n+  if (options.planVersion) {\n+    content += `- **Plan Version**: ${options.planVersion}\\n`;\n+  }\n+  \n+  // Add file context\n+  if (options.context && options.context.length > 0) {\n+    const contextStr = options.context\n+      .map(c => c.startLine \n+        ? `${c.file}:${c.startLine}-${c.endLine || c.startLine}`\n+        : c.file\n+      )\n+      .join(', ');\n+    content += `- **Related Files**: ${contextStr}\\n`;\n+  }\n+  \n+  // What was proposed\n+  if (options.proposed) {\n+    content += `\n+## What Was Proposed\n+\n+${options.proposed}\n+`;\n+  }\n+  \n+  // Feedback given\n+  content += `\n+## Feedback Given\n+\n+${options.feedback}\n+`;\n+  \n+  // Discussion\n+  if (options.discussion) {\n+    content += `\n+## Discussion\n+\n+${options.discussion}\n+`;\n+  }\n+  \n+  // Resolution\n+  if (options.resolution) {\n+    content += `\n+## Resolution\n+\n+${options.resolution}\n+`;\n+  }\n+  \n+  // Changes made\n+  if (options.changes && options.changes.length > 0) {\n+    content += `\n+## Changes Made\n+\n+${options.changes.map(c => `- ${c}`).join('\\n')}\n+`;\n+  }\n+  \n+  return content;\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 4. Implement createFeedback Function\n+\n+```typescript\n+export interface CreateFeedbackResult {\n+  /** Created feedback record */\n+  record: FeedbackRecord;\n+  \n+  /** Path to created file */\n+  path: string;\n+}\n+\n+export async function createFeedback(\n+  planPath: string,\n+  options: CreateFeedbackOptions\n+): Promise<CreateFeedbackResult> {\n+  // Ensure feedback directory exists\n+  const feedbackDir = join(planPath, PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardDirs.feedback);\n+  await mkdir(feedbackDir, { recursive: true });\n+  \n+  // Generate ID and filename\n+  const id = await getNextFeedbackId(feedbackDir);\n+  const slug = slugify(options.title);\n+  const filename = `${id}-${slug}.md`;\n+  const filePath = join(feedbackDir, filename);\n+  \n+  // Generate content\n+  const content = generateFeedbackMd(id, options);\n+  \n+  // Write file\n+  await writeFile(filePath, content);\n+  \n+  // Construct record\n+  const record: FeedbackRecord = {\n+    id,\n+    title: options.title,\n+    createdAt: new Date(),\n+    participants: options.participants || [],\n+    platform: options.platform || 'other',\n+    feedback: options.feedback,\n+    proposed: options.proposed,\n+    discussion: options.discussion,\n+    resolution: options.resolution,\n+    changes: options.changes,\n+    context: options.context,\n+    planVersion: options.planVersion,\n+    filename\n+  };\n+  \n+  return { record, path: filePath };\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 5. Implement listFeedback Function\n+\n+```typescript\n+export async function listFeedback(\n+  planPath: string\n+): Promise<FeedbackRecord[]> {\n+  const feedbackDir = join(planPath, PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardDirs.feedback);\n+  \n+  try {\n+    const files = await readdir(feedbackDir);\n+    const records: FeedbackRecord[] = [];\n+    \n+    for (const file of files) {\n+      if (!PLAN_CONVENTIONS.feedbackPattern.test(file)) continue;\n+      \n+      const content = await readFile(join(feedbackDir, file), 'utf-8');\n+      const record = parseFeedbackFile(file, content);\n+      if (record) records.push(record);\n+    }\n+    \n+    return records.sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id));\n+  } catch {\n+    return []; // No feedback directory\n+  }\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 6. Implement getFeedback Function\n+\n+```typescript\n+export async function getFeedback(\n+  planPath: string,\n+  id: string\n+): Promise<FeedbackRecord | null> {\n+  const feedbackDir = join(planPath, PLAN_CONVENTIONS.standardDirs.feedback);\n+  \n+  try {\n+    const files = await readdir(feedbackDir);\n+    \n+    for (const file of files) {\n+      const match = file.match(PLAN_CONVENTIONS.feedbackPattern);\n+      if (match && match[1] === id.padStart(3, '0')) {\n+        const content = await readFile(join(feedbackDir, file), 'utf-8');\n+        return parseFeedbackFile(file, content);\n+      }\n+    }\n+    \n+    return null;\n+  } catch {\n+    return null;\n+  }\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 7. Implement Feedback Parser\n+\n+```typescript\n+function parseFeedbackFile(\n+  filename: string, \n+  content: string\n+): FeedbackRecord | null {\n+  const match = filename.match(PLAN_CONVENTIONS.feedbackPattern);\n+  if (!match) return null;\n+  \n+  const [, id] = match;\n+  \n+  // Extract title from first heading\n+  const titleMatch = content.match(/^#\\s+Feedback:\\s+(.+)$/m);\n+  const title = titleMatch ? titleMatch[1].trim() : 'Untitled';\n+  \n+  // Extract date from context\n+  const dateMatch = content.match(/\\*\\*Date\\*\\*:\\s*(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2})/);\n+  const createdAt = dateMatch ? new Date(dateMatch[1]) : new Date();\n+  \n+  // Extract platform\n+  const platformMatch = content.match(/\\*\\*Platform\\*\\*:\\s*(\\w+)/);\n+  const platform = platformMatch \n+    ? (platformMatch[1].toLowerCase() as FeedbackPlatform) \n+    : 'other';\n+  \n+  // Extract participants (simplified)\n+  const participants: FeedbackParticipant[] = [];\n+  const participantsMatch = content.match(/\\*\\*Participants\\*\\*:\\s*(.+)/);\n+  if (participantsMatch) {\n+    // Parse participant string (e.g., \"Tim (human), Claude (ai)\")\n+    const parts = participantsMatch[1].split(',');\n+    for (const part of parts) {\n+      const pMatch = part.trim().match(/(.+?)\\s*\\((\\w+)(?:,\\s*(.+))?\\)/);\n+      if (pMatch) {\n+        participants.push({\n+          name: pMatch[1].trim(),\n+          type: pMatch[2] as 'human' | 'ai',\n+          model: pMatch[3]?.trim()\n+        });\n+      }\n+    }\n+  }\n+  \n+  // Extract feedback section\n+  const feedbackSection = extractSection(content, 'Feedback Given');\n+  \n+  // Extract other sections\n+  const proposed = extractSection(content, 'What Was Proposed');\n+  const discussion = extractSection(content, 'Discussion');\n+  const resolution = extractSection(content, 'Resolution');\n+  \n+  return {\n+    id,\n+    title,\n+    createdAt,\n+    participants,\n+    platform,\n+    feedback: feedbackSection || content,\n+    proposed,\n+    discussion,\n+    resolution,\n+    filename\n+  };\n+}\n+\n+function extractSection(content: string, sectionName: string): string | undefined {\n+  const regex = new RegExp(`##\\\\s+${sectionName}\\\\s*\\\\n([\\\\s\\\\S]*?)(?=\\\\n##|$)`, 'i');\n+  const match = content.match(regex);\n+  return match ? match[1].trim() : undefined;\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 8. Update Index Exports\n+\n+In `src/index.ts`:\n+\n+```typescript\n+export {\n+  createFeedback,\n+  listFeedback,\n+  getFeedback,\n+  type CreateFeedbackOptions,\n+  type CreateFeedbackResult\n+} from './feedback/index.js';\n+```\n+\n+## Acceptance Criteria\n+\n+- [ ] `createFeedback()` creates properly formatted feedback files\n+- [ ] Feedback IDs auto-increment (001, 002, 003...)\n+- [ ] Filenames are valid and slugified\n+- [ ] `listFeedback()` returns all feedback records sorted by ID\n+- [ ] `getFeedback(id)` retrieves specific record\n+- [ ] Feedback files can be parsed back into records\n+- [ ] Works with plans that don't have feedback/ yet\n+- [ ] **`npm run precommit` passes** ← Definition of Done\n+\n+## Testing\n+\n+```typescript\n+// tests/feedback.test.ts\n+import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from 'vitest';\n+import { createPlan, createFeedback, listFeedback, getFeedback } from '../src/index';\n+import { rm } from 'node:fs/promises';\n+import { join } from 'node:path';\n+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';\n+\n+describe('Feedback', () => {\n+  let testDir: string;\n+  let planPath: string;\n+  \n+  beforeEach(async () => {\n+    testDir = join(tmpdir(), `riotplan-feedback-test-${Date.now()}`);\n+    const result = await createPlan({\n+      code: 'test-plan',\n+      name: 'Test Plan',\n+      basePath: testDir\n+    });\n+    planPath = result.path;\n+  });\n+  \n+  afterEach(async () => {\n+    await rm(testDir, { recursive: true });\n+  });\n+  \n+  describe('createFeedback', () => {\n+    it('should create a feedback file', async () => {\n+      const result = await createFeedback(planPath, {\n+        title: 'Initial Review',\n+        feedback: 'Consider adding more steps',\n+        participants: [\n+          { name: 'Tim', type: 'human' },\n+          { name: 'Claude', type: 'ai', model: 'claude-sonnet-4-20250514' }\n+        ],\n+        platform: 'cursor'\n+      });\n+      \n+      expect(result.record.id).toBe('001');\n+      expect(result.record.title).toBe('Initial Review');\n+    });\n+    \n+    it('should auto-increment IDs', async () => {\n+      await createFeedback(planPath, { title: 'First', feedback: 'Feedback 1' });\n+      await createFeedback(planPath, { title: 'Second', feedback: 'Feedback 2' });\n+      const result = await createFeedback(planPath, { title: 'Third', feedback: 'Feedback 3' });\n+      \n+      expect(result.record.id).toBe('003');\n+    });\n+  });\n+  \n+  describe('listFeedback', () => {\n+    it('should list all feedback', async () => {\n+      await createFeedback(planPath, { title: 'First', feedback: 'F1' });\n+      await createFeedback(planPath, { title: 'Second', feedback: 'F2' });\n+      \n+      const records = await listFeedback(planPath);\n+      \n+      expect(records.length).toBe(2);\n+      expect(records[0].id).toBe('001');\n+      expect(records[1].id).toBe('002');\n+    });\n+    \n+    it('should return empty array for plan without feedback', async () => {\n+      const newPlanResult = await createPlan({\n+        code: 'no-feedback',\n+        name: 'No Feedback',\n+        basePath: testDir,\n+        createFeedbackDir: false\n+      });\n+      \n+      const records = await listFeedback(newPlanResult.path);\n+      expect(records).toEqual([]);\n+    });\n+  });\n+  \n+  describe('getFeedback', () => {\n+    it('should get specific feedback', async () => {\n+      await createFeedback(planPath, { title: 'Test', feedback: 'Content' });\n+      \n+      const record = await getFeedback(planPath, '001');\n+      \n+      expect(record).not.toBeNull();\n+      expect(record!.title).toBe('Test');\n+    });\n+    \n+    it('should return null for non-existent ID', async () => {\n+      const record = await getFeedback(planPath, '999');\n+      expect(record).toBeNull();\n+    });\n+  });\n+});\n+```\n+\n+## Files Changed\n+\n+- `riotplan/src/feedback/index.ts` - New file\n+- `riotplan/src/index.ts` - Export feedback functions\n+- `riotplan/tests/feedback.test.ts` - Tests\n+\n+## Notes\n+\n+- This is the **philosophical foundation** of RiotPlan\n+- Feedback files are human-readable markdown\n+- Parser is forgiving of variations in format\n+- Consider adding `updateFeedback()` and `deleteFeedback()` later\n+\n+## [CHANGE OMITTED: Would exceed total size limit] ...\n\ndiff --git a/prompts/implement-riotplan/plan/13-internal-dependencies.md b/prompts/implement-riotplan/plan/13-internal-dependencies.md\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..69cdc41\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/prompts/implement-riotplan/plan/13-internal-dependencies.md\n@@ -0,0 +1,84 @@\n+# Step 13: Internal Dependencies\n+\n+## Objective\n+\n+Implement within-plan dependency tracking: steps can depend on other steps, and the system validates and respects these dependencies.\n+\n+## Background\n+\n+Steps often have dependencies: Step 5 can't start until Steps 1-4 are complete. This step implements:\n+\n+- Dependency declaration in step files\n+- Dependency validation (no circular, valid references)\n+- Status propagation (blocked if dependency blocked)\n+- \"Ready to execute\" calculation\n+\n+## Tasks\n+\n+### 1. Add Dependency Parsing to Step Loading\n+\n+Extend step loading to parse dependency declarations from step file headers:\n+\n+```markdown\n+# Step 05: Implementation\n+\n+**Dependencies**: 01, 02, 03, 04\n+```\n+\n+### 2. Implement Dependency Validation\n+\n+```typescript\n+export function validateDependencies(steps: PlanStep[]): ValidationResult {\n+  // Check: all dependencies exist\n+  // Check: no self-dependencies\n+  // Check: no circular dependencies (using topological sort)\n+  // Check: forward dependencies are warned\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 3. Implement Ready-to-Execute Logic\n+\n+```typescript\n+export function getReadySteps(plan: Plan): PlanStep[] {\n+  return plan.steps.filter(step => {\n+    // Pending status\n+    if (step.status !== 'pending') return false;\n+    \n+    // All dependencies completed\n+    if (step.dependencies) {\n+      for (const depNum of step.dependencies) {\n+        const dep = plan.steps.find(s => s.number === depNum);\n+        if (!dep || dep.status !== 'completed') return false;\n+      }\n+    }\n+    \n+    return true;\n+  });\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 4. Add CLI Integration\n+\n+- `riotplan status` shows blocked steps with reasons\n+- `riotplan step ready` lists steps ready to execute\n+- Dependency visualization in dashboard\n+\n+## Acceptance Criteria\n+\n+- [ ] Dependencies parsed from step files\n+- [ ] Circular dependencies detected\n+- [ ] `getReadySteps()` returns correct steps\n+- [ ] Status commands show dependency info\n+- [ ] **`npm run precommit` passes** ← Definition of Done\n+\n+## Files Changed\n+\n+- `riotplan/src/steps/dependencies.ts` - New module\n+- `riotplan/src/plan/loader.ts` - Parse dependencies\n+- `riotplan-cli/src/commands/step/ready.ts` - New command\n+\n+## Dependencies\n+\n+- Step 02 (Plan Loading)\n+- Step 07 (Step Operations)\n+\ndiff --git a/prompts/implement-riotplan/plan/14-external-dependencies.md b/prompts/implement-riotplan/plan/14-external-dependencies.md\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..3f23f3e\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/prompts/implement-riotplan/plan/14-external-dependencies.md\n@@ -0,0 +1,103 @@\n+# Step 14: External Dependencies\n+\n+## Objective\n+\n+Implement cross-plan dependencies: steps can depend on external plans, and status propagates when external plans complete.\n+\n+## Background\n+\n+From RIOTPLAN_ANALYSIS.md: Plans have dependencies spanning repositories:\n+\n+```\n+kjerneverk/secure-riotprompt Step 7\n+  └── BLOCKED on fjellgrunn/security-logging-features\n+```\n+\n+This step implements:\n+\n+- External plan references\n+- Link creation and management\n+- Status synchronization\n+\n+## Tasks\n+\n+### 1. Add External Dependency Types\n+\n+```typescript\n+export interface ExternalDependency {\n+  /** Type of external reference */\n+  type: 'plan' | 'step';\n+  \n+  /** Path to external plan (relative or absolute) */\n+  planPath: string;\n+  \n+  /** Specific step number (if type is 'step') */\n+  stepNumber?: number;\n+  \n+  /** Description of dependency */\n+  reason?: string;\n+  \n+  /** Current status of external plan/step */\n+  status?: TaskStatus;\n+  \n+  /** When last checked */\n+  lastChecked?: Date;\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 2. Implement Link Management\n+\n+```typescript\n+export async function linkPlan(\n+  plan: Plan,\n+  stepNumber: number,\n+  externalPlanPath: string,\n+  options?: { reason?: string; stepNumber?: number }\n+): Promise<void>;\n+\n+export async function unlinkPlan(\n+  plan: Plan,\n+  stepNumber: number,\n+  externalPlanPath: string\n+): Promise<void>;\n+\n+export async function syncExternalDependencies(\n+  plan: Plan\n+): Promise<SyncResult>;\n+```\n+\n+### 3. Add CLI Commands\n+\n+```bash\n+riotplan link <step> <path>      # Link external plan\n+riotplan unlink <step> <path>    # Remove link\n+riotplan sync                    # Sync all external deps\n+```\n+\n+### 4. Implement Status Propagation\n+\n+When syncing:\n+- Load external plan\n+- Check status of linked step/plan\n+- Update local step status if needed\n+- Report changes\n+\n+## Acceptance Criteria\n+\n+- [ ] Can link steps to external plans\n+- [ ] `riotplan sync` updates status from external plans\n+- [ ] Blocked status propagates correctly\n+- [ ] Clear output showing external dependencies\n+- [ ] **`npm run precommit` passes** ← Definition of Done\n+\n+## Files Changed\n+\n+- `riotplan/src/dependencies/external.ts` - New module\n+- `riotplan-cli/src/commands/link.ts` - New command\n+- `riotplan-cli/src/commands/sync.ts` - New command\n+\n+## Dependencies\n+\n+- Step 13 (Internal Dependencies)\n+- Step 02 (Plan Loading)\n+\ndiff --git a/prompts/implement-riotplan/plan/15-plan-registry.md b/prompts/implement-riotplan/plan/15-plan-registry.md\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..c1b37aa\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/prompts/implement-riotplan/plan/15-plan-registry.md\n@@ -0,0 +1,115 @@\n+# Step 15: Plan Registry\n+\n+## Objective\n+\n+Implement a plan registry to track plans across multiple directories and repositories, enabling cross-project coordination.\n+\n+## Background\n+\n+Users manage plans across multiple locations:\n+- `~/gitw/kjerneverk/prompts/`\n+- `~/gitw/fjellgrunn/prompts/`\n+- `~/projects/personal/plans/`\n+\n+The registry provides:\n+- Central index of all plans\n+- Quick lookup without scanning\n+- Metadata caching\n+- Cross-repo awareness\n+\n+## Tasks\n+\n+### 1. Design Registry Format\n+\n+```typescript\n+// ~/.config/riotplan/registry.json\n+export interface PlanRegistry {\n+  version: string;\n+  lastUpdated: Date;\n+  \n+  /** Registered scan paths */\n+  scanPaths: string[];\n+  \n+  /** Known plans */\n+  plans: RegisteredPlan[];\n+  \n+  /** Contexts */\n+  contexts: ContextDefinition[];\n+}\n+\n+export interface RegisteredPlan {\n+  /** Plan code */\n+  code: string;\n+  \n+  /** Absolute path */\n+  path: string;\n+  \n+  /** Plan name */\n+  name: string;\n+  \n+  /** Current status (cached) */\n+  status: TaskStatus;\n+  \n+  /** Progress (cached) */\n+  progress: number;\n+  \n+  /** Context assignment */\n+  context?: string;\n+  \n+  /** Last scanned */\n+  lastScanned: Date;\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 2. Implement Registry Management\n+\n+```typescript\n+export async function getRegistry(): Promise<PlanRegistry>;\n+export async function saveRegistry(registry: PlanRegistry): Promise<void>;\n+\n+export async function scanPath(path: string): Promise<RegisteredPlan[]>;\n+export async function refreshRegistry(): Promise<RefreshResult>;\n+\n+export async function registerPlan(planPath: string): Promise<void>;\n+export async function unregisterPlan(planPath: string): Promise<void>;\n+```\n+\n+### 3.
